MQL5 ZigZag distribution indicator debugging

Created 2026-01-09 17:54 UTC · Last updated 2026-01-09 22:30 UTC · 149 messages
Original: claude.ai/chat/016f5d1e-5dff-4cec-8185-1b17558def06
Millard2026-01-09 17:54 UTC
We need to fix this code, MQL5 indicator. Requirements: this MQL5 indicator is supposed to build a distribution of ZigZag lines by spread (as opposed to true length which would consider the time axis on the chart). It's supposed to keep this distribution current as new bars form on the chart. It should not recalculate historical time periods/bars once the initial history processing is finished. It should not repaint the indicator for historical bars. It should use the most recent ZZ data available and, if necessary, calculate its own internal "ZZ in-work" leg (segment) if the actual indicator hasn't painted on yet. It should compare the latest ZZ leg to the selected percentile from the distribution and come up with a percentage, current leg / max value from the percentile. It should plot the indicator to show that value. Color will also be important, but for now I want to focus on getting the line itself accurate. Find all the problems you can in this code that diverge from these requirements. Problems found by another AI: The MQL5 indicator attempts time-correct processing but has multiple flaws preventing reliable non-repainting spread-based ZigZag distribution tracking. Key divergences from requirements include improper spread measurement, distribution contamination, and incomplete in-work leg handling. Core Measurement Flaw The code measures ZigZag endpoint-to-endpoint distances (pivot prices) rather than true spread (considering time axis via bar count or duration). Requirement demands spread distribution where spread = f(price_diff, time_span), but `MaybeHandleEndpointEvent()` and `ConfirmPivot()` use only `MathAbs(ep_price - g_last_ep_price) / pip_size` without time component. Distribution Recalculation Historical distributions rebuild on every `OnCalculate()` call via `UpdatePivots(rates_total)` which fully re-extracts all ZigZag pivots from buffer 0. This ignores `prev_calculated` optimization and recalculates `Leg_Dist[]` from potentially repainted historical ZigZag data, violating "no recalculation of historical periods" requirement. ZigZag Repainting Not Mitigated Code excludes only last 2 pivots (`confirmed_pivots = zz_pivot_count - 2`) from distribution, but ZigZag can repaint much deeper (up to `ZZ_Depth` bars back). Historical legs remain contaminated by unconfirmed ZigZag values that shift as new bars form. In-Work Leg Incomplete Current simulated leg (`UpdateSimInWorkForBar()`) tracks running high/low extremes but doesn't persist across full OnCalculate cycles. `ResetSimInWork()` and endpoint state resets lose in-work tracking when ZigZag lags, causing gaps until next endpoint throws. Percentage Calculation Wrong Plots `100.0 * (ext_pips / target_percentile)` where `target = GetPercentileValue(Leg_Dist, ..., ConfidenceTargetPercentile)`. Requirement specifies "current leg / max value from the percentile" - unclear if code's percentile value equals "max value from percentile bin," but likely diverges. State Persistence Broken Critical state (`g_pivot_confirmed_flags[]`, `Leg_Dist[]`, endpoint tracking) resets on indicator reinit (`ResetTimeCorrectState()` in `prev_calculated==0`). Distribution rebuilds from scratch each chart attach/timeframe change, destroying incremental requirement. No True Non-Repainting Guarantee Despite complex "time-correct" logic, `UpdatePivots()` always refreshes full `zz_raw[]` buffer. Historical bar calculations see current (possibly repainted) ZigZag values rather than frozen values at bar close time, enabling subtle repaints. Here's the code:
Attached file: unnamed

int    g_cp2_bar = -1; double g_cp2_price = 0.0;

//--- Running totals (pips) for mock trades
double g_cum_pl = 0.0;
double g_cum_gain = 0.0;
double g_cum_loss = 0.0; // negative

//--- In-work leg simulation for confidence line (derived from ZZ confirmed pivots)
// Direction: +1 = up leg (tracking highest high), -1 = down leg (tracking lowest low), 0 = unknown
int    g_inwork_dir = 0;
double g_inwork_extreme = 0.0;

void ResetInWork()
{
   g_inwork_dir = 0;
   g_inwork_extreme = 0.0;
}

void RecomputeInWorkDirFromLastPivots()
{
   // Need at least 2 confirmed pivots to know whether the last pivot is a high or low.
   if(g_confirmed_pivot_seq < 2) { g_inwork_dir = 0; return; }
   // If last pivot is higher than previous, last pivot is a HIGH -> current leg direction is DOWN.
   // If last pivot is lower than previous, last pivot is a LOW -> current leg direction is UP.
   if(g_cp2_price > g_cp1_price) g_inwork_dir = -1;
   else if(g_cp2_price < g_cp1_price) g_inwork_dir = 1;
   else g_inwork_dir = 0;
}

void UpdateInWorkExtremeForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   // Deprecated: replaced by simulated in-work leg below (kept to avoid large diff churn)
}

//--- ZigZag endpoint tracking (buffer 0 "latest non-zero up to bar")
double g_last_ep_price = 0.0;
int    g_last_ep_pos = -1;
double g_prev_ep_price = 0.0;
int    g_prev_ep_pos = -1;
bool   g_have_ep = false;

//--- Simulated in-work leg (continuous, fixes "boxy" and direction-staleness)
// Leg start is the previous endpoint price (g_prev_ep_price).
double g_sim_start_price = 0.0;
double g_sim_max_high = 0.0;
double g_sim_min_low = 0.0;
int    g_sim_dir = 0;         // +1 up, -1 down
double g_sim_extreme = 0.0;   // max_high (up) or min_low (down)

void ResetSimInWork()
{
   g_sim_start_price = 0.0;
   g_sim_max_high = 0.0;
   g_sim_min_low = 0.0;
   g_sim_dir = 0;
   g_sim_extreme = 0.0;
}

void UpdateSimInWorkForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_prev_ep_pos < 0) return;

   // Reset when leg start changes
   if(g_sim_start_price != g_prev_ep_price || g_sim_extreme == 0.0)
   {
      g_sim_start_price = g_prev_ep_price;
      g_sim_max_high = g_prev_ep_price;
      g_sim_min_low = g_prev_ep_price;
      g_sim_dir = 0;
      g_sim_extreme = g_prev_ep_price;
   }

   if(high[bar] > g_sim_max_high) g_sim_max_high = high[bar];
   if(low[bar] < g_sim_min_low) g_sim_min_low = low[bar];

   double up_ext = g_sim_max_high - g_sim_start_price;
   double dn_ext = g_sim_start_price - g_sim_min_low;

   if(up_ext >= dn_ext) { g_sim_dir = 1; g_sim_extreme = g_sim_max_high; }
   else { g_sim_dir = -1; g_sim_extreme = g_sim_min_low; }
}

bool GetLatestEndpointUpToBar(int bar, double &price, int &pos)
{
   if(bar < 0) return false;
   int n = ArraySize(zz_raw);
   if(n <= 0) return false;
   if(bar >= n) bar = n - 1;
   for(int i = bar; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      double v = zz_raw[i];
      if(v != 0.0)
      {
         price = v;
         pos = i;
         return true;
      }
   }
   return false;
}

void ResetEndpointState()
{
   g_last_ep_price = 0.0;
   g_last_ep_pos = -1;
   g_prev_ep_price = 0.0;
   g_prev_ep_pos = -1;
   g_have_ep = false;
   ResetSimInWork();
}

// Handle endpoint changes as "pivot events" at the *current bar* being processed (never rewrite past bars)
void MaybeHandleEndpointEvent(int bar, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[], const double &close[], int rates_total, double pip_size)
{
   double ep_price = 0.0;
   int ep_pos = -1;
   if(!GetLatestEndpointUpToBar(bar, ep_price, ep_pos))
      return;

   if(!g_have_ep)
   {
      g_last_ep_price = ep_price;
      g_last_ep_pos = ep_pos;
      g_have_ep = true;
      return;
   }

   if(ep_price == g_last_ep_price && ep_pos == g_last_ep_pos)
      return; // no new endpoint thrown

   // If we don't yet have a "previous endpoint", this is the first completed leg; initialize direction and endpoints.
   if(g_prev_ep_pos < 0)
   {
      g_prev_ep_price = g_last_ep_price;
      g_prev_ep_pos   = g_last_ep_pos;
      g_last_ep_price = ep_price;
      g_last_ep_pos   = ep_pos;

      // Direction of the current leg is from previous->last
      double delta0 = g_last_ep_price - g_prev_ep_price;
      g_inwork_dir = (delta0 > 0) ? 1 : (delta0 < 0 ? -1 : 0);
      g_has_leg_start = (g_inwork_dir != 0);
      // Reset simulated in-work leg now that we have a leg start
      ResetSimInWork();

      return;
   }

   // We already have a current direction. Decide whether this is an extension or a pivot.
   double delta = ep_price - g_last_ep_price;
   if(delta == 0.0)
      return;

   // User rule: if (delta * dir) > 0 => extension; if < 0 => pivot (direction flips)
   double signed_move = delta * (double)g_inwork_dir;

   if(g_inwork_dir == 0)
   {
      // If direction unknown, adopt the delta direction as the current leg direction and treat as extension.
      g_inwork_dir = (delta > 0) ? 1 : -1;
      g_last_ep_price = ep_price;
      g_last_ep_pos = ep_pos;
      return;
   }

   if(signed_move > 0.0)
   {
      // Extension: endpoint moved further in the same direction; update the current endpoint only.
      g_last_ep_price = ep_price;
      g_last_ep_pos = ep_pos;
      // Do not reset simulated in-work; it continues tracking extremes.
      return;
   }

   // Pivot: endpoint moved opposite direction; treat THIS bar as the pivot-event bar (no retro writes).
   // Completed leg size is between the previous endpoint (pivot) and the current endpoint (pivot).
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(ep_price - g_last_ep_price) / pip_size;
   if(leg_size_pips > 0)
      InsertSorted(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, leg_size_pips);

   // Shift endpoints: previous becomes old last, last becomes new
   g_prev_ep_price = g_last_ep_price;
   g_prev_ep_pos   = g_last_ep_pos;
   g_last_ep_price = ep_price;
   g_last_ep_pos   = ep_pos;

   // Flip direction
   g_inwork_dir = -g_inwork_dir;
   g_has_leg_start = (g_inwork_dir != 0);
   // New leg start; reset simulated in-work
   ResetSimInWork();

   // When we have enough legs, set the first bar where output becomes meaningful (time-correct)
   if(indicator_start_bar == 0 && Leg_Dist_Count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
      indicator_start_bar = bar;

   // When we have enough legs, set the first bar where output becomes meaningful (time-correct)
   // #region agent log (low-noise)
   Print("HC endpoint event @bar=", bar,
         " ep_pos=", ep_pos,
         " ep=", DoubleToString(ep_price, _Digits),
         " dir=", g_inwork_dir,
         " dist_count=", Leg_Dist_Count);
   // #endregion

   // Trades (same rule: never write into past) are handled elsewhere; this event only updates indicator state.
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ResetTimeCorrectState(int rates_total)
{
   ArrayResize(g_pivot_confirmed_flags, rates_total);
   ArrayInitialize(g_pivot_confirmed_flags, false);
   g_has_leg_start = false;
   g_leg_start_pivot_bar = -1;
   g_leg_start_price = 0.0;
   g_prev_confirmed_pivot_price = 0.0;
   g_have_prev_confirmed = false;

   ArrayResize(Leg_Dist, 0);
   Leg_Dist_Count = 0;
   indicator_start_bar = 0;

   g_last_processed_closed_bar = -1;
   g_cp0_bar = -1; g_cp0_price = 0.0;
   g_cp1_bar = -1; g_cp1_price = 0.0;
   g_cp2_bar = -1; g_cp2_price = 0.0;
   g_confirmed_pivot_seq = 0;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   ResetInWork();
   ResetEndpointState();
}

void MaybePlaceMockTradeFromConfirmedPivots(const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[], const double &close[], int rates_total)
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_confirmed_pivot_seq < 3) return; // need 3 confirmed pivots to classify the middle one

   // We place a trade for the middle pivot (cp1), exiting at cp2.
   int entry_pivot_bar = g_cp1_bar;
   int exit_pivot_bar  = g_cp2_bar;
   if(entry_pivot_bar < 0 || exit_pivot_bar < 0) return;
   if(entry_pivot_bar >= rates_total || exit_pivot_bar >= rates_total) return;
   if(exit_pivot_bar <= entry_pivot_bar) return;

   // Trade_Start_Date gating only applies during initial build.
   if(g_in_initial_build)
   {
      if(Trade_Start_Date == 0) return;
      if(time[entry_pivot_bar] < Trade_Start_Date) return;
   }

   // Determine direction based on whether entry pivot is a trough vs previous confirmed pivot.
   bool is_trough = (g_cp1_price < g_cp0_price);

   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double trade_price = close[entry_pivot_bar];
   double exit_price  = close[exit_pivot_bar];
   double pl_pips = is_trough ? (exit_price - trade_price) / pip_size
                              : (trade_price - exit_price) / pip_size;

   // Arrow placed on next bar after entry pivot (trade executes after bar closes)
   int arrow_bar = entry_pivot_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];

   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * pip_size;
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips  * pip_size;

   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_pivot_bar) + "_" + IntegerToString(exit_pivot_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;

   if(ObjectFind(0, arrow_name) >= 0 || ObjectFind(0, label_name) >= 0)
      return; // already placed

   double arrow_price;
   double label_price;
   if(is_trough)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - label_offset;
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + label_offset;
   }

   // Label formatting: gains as plain numbers; losses in parentheses (bookkeeping style)
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0 ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs);
   color pl_color = is_trough ? clrLime : clrRed;

   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);

   // Alerts only after initial build completes
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || realtime)
      {
         string side = is_trough ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence MJM: ", side, " arrow placed @ ", _Symbol, " ", EnumToString(_Period),
               " time=", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
               " price=", DoubleToString(trade_price, _Digits));
      }
   }

   // Update running totals (pips)
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0) g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else g_cum_loss += pl_pips; // negative

   Print("HC TRADE: entry_pivot=", entry_pivot_bar, " exit_pivot=", exit_pivot_bar, " pl_pips=", DoubleToString(pl_pips, 0),
         " (initial_build=", (int)g_in_initial_build, ")");
}

// Confirm a pivot (whose bar is sufficiently old) and update distributions.
void ConfirmPivot(int pivot_bar, double pivot_price, double pip_size)
{
   if(pivot_bar < 0) return;
   if(pivot_bar >= ArraySize(g_pivot_confirmed_flags)) return;
   if(g_pivot_confirmed_flags[pivot_bar]) return;

   g_pivot_confirmed_flags[pivot_bar] = true;

   if(!g_have_prev_confirmed)
   {
      g_prev_confirmed_pivot_price = pivot_price;
      g_have_prev_confirmed = true;
   }
   else
   {
      double leg_size = MathAbs(pivot_price - g_prev_confirmed_pivot_price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size > 0)
         InsertSorted(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, leg_size);
      g_prev_confirmed_pivot_price = pivot_price;
   }

   // New leg starts from this pivot (for subsequent bars)
   g_has_leg_start = true;
   g_leg_start_pivot_bar = pivot_bar;
   g_leg_start_price = pivot_price;

   // Track last 3 confirmed pivots for mock trades
   if(g_confirmed_pivot_seq == 0)
   {
      g_cp2_bar = pivot_bar; g_cp2_price = pivot_price;
   }
   else if(g_confirmed_pivot_seq == 1)
   {
      g_cp1_bar = g_cp2_bar; g_cp1_price = g_cp2_price;
      g_cp2_bar = pivot_bar; g_cp2_price = pivot_price;
   }
   else
   {
      g_cp0_bar = g_cp1_bar; g_cp0_price = g_cp1_price;
      g_cp1_bar = g_cp2_bar; g_cp1_price = g_cp2_price;
      g_cp2_bar = pivot_bar; g_cp2_price = pivot_price;
   }
   g_confirmed_pivot_seq++;

   // Update in-work direction and reset in-work extreme on pivot confirmation
   RecomputeInWorkDirFromLastPivots();
   g_inwork_extreme = g_leg_start_price;

   // #region agent log (low-noise)
   Print("HC pivot confirmed: bar=", pivot_bar,
         " price=", DoubleToString(pivot_price, _Digits),
         " inwork_dir=", g_inwork_dir);
   // #endregion
}

// Called when a bar closes (in initial build, we treat bars sequentially as closing; in live, we call on new-bar).
void MaybeConfirmPivotOnBarClose(int closed_bar, int rates_total, double pip_size)
{
   int pivot_candidate_bar = closed_bar - ZZ_Depth;
   if(pivot_candidate_bar < 0) return;
   if(pivot_candidate_bar >= rates_total) return;
   if(ArraySize(zz_raw) != rates_total) return;

   double pivot_price = zz_raw[pivot_candidate_bar];
   if(pivot_price != 0.0)
      ConfirmPivot(pivot_candidate_bar, pivot_price, pip_size);

   // When we have enough legs, set the first bar where output becomes meaningful (time-correct)
   if(indicator_start_bar == 0 && Leg_Dist_Count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
      indicator_start_bar = closed_bar;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   SetIndexBuffer(0, Historic_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX);
   
   //--- Debug buffers (INDICATOR_DATA with DRAW_NONE = visible in Data Window but not plotted)
   SetIndexBuffer(3, Debug_PivotIdx, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(4, Debug_LegSize, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(5, Debug_LegStart, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(6, Debug_LegDistCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(7, Debug_PivotCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(8, CumulativePL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(9, CumulativeGain, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   // Plot indices: 0=Historic, 1=Combined(+color), 2-7=Debug
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   
   PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotIdx");
   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize");
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "LegStart");
   PlotIndexSetString(5, PLOT_LABEL, "DistCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(6, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativePL");
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeGain");
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeLoss (neg)");
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, Historic_Show ? DRAW_LINE : DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Historic_Width);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_COLOR_INDEXES, 2);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("Error creating ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return(INIT_FAILED);
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM");
   distributions_built = false;
   csv_exported = false;
   trades_calculated = false;
   last_bar_processed = 0;
   last_pivot_count = 0;
   indicator_start_bar = 0;
   stats_printed = false;

   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_current_bar_time = 0;
   g_trades_initialized = false;
   g_next_trade_pivot = 0;
   g_in_initial_build = false;
   g_cp0_bar = -1; g_cp0_price = 0.0;
   g_cp1_bar = -1; g_cp1_price = 0.0;
   g_cp2_bar = -1; g_cp2_price = 0.0;
   g_confirmed_pivot_seq = 0;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   
   // Remove any existing trade objects from previous runs
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   return(INIT_SUCCEEDED);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   // Clean up trade objects
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Format number with comma separators                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Insert value into sorted double array                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update pivot arrays from current ZigZag state                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdatePivots(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0) return;

   ArrayResize(zz_raw, rates_total);
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
      zz_raw[i] = zz_buffer[i];
   
   ArrayResize(zz_pivots, 0);
   ArrayResize(zz_pivot_bars, 0);
   zz_pivot_count = 0;
   
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0)
      {
         ArrayResize(zz_pivots, zz_pivot_count + 1);
         ArrayResize(zz_pivot_bars, zz_pivot_count + 1);
         zz_pivots[zz_pivot_count] = zz_buffer[i];
         zz_pivot_bars[zz_pivot_count] = i;
         zz_pivot_count++;
      }
   }

   // #region agent log (one-time-ish per rebuild, Experts tab)
   // Print a compact signature of pivot extraction so we can compare across charts/timeframe switches.
   static int s_last_sig_pivot_count = -1;
   static int s_last_sig_rates_total = -1;
   if(zz_pivot_count > 0 && (zz_pivot_count != s_last_sig_pivot_count || rates_total != s_last_sig_rates_total))
   {
      int last = zz_pivot_count - 1;
      int a0 = 0;
      int a1 = (zz_pivot_count > 1 ? 1 : 0);
      int a2 = (zz_pivot_count > 2 ? 2 : a1);
      int b0 = (zz_pivot_count > 3 ? last - 2 : 0);
      int b1 = (zz_pivot_count > 2 ? last - 1 : a1);
      int b2 = last;

      Print("HC ZZ pivots sig: rates_total=", rates_total,
            " pivots=", zz_pivot_count,
            " bars[0..2]=", zz_pivot_bars[a0], ",", zz_pivot_bars[a1], ",", zz_pivot_bars[a2],
            " bars[last-2..last]=", zz_pivot_bars[b0], ",", zz_pivot_bars[b1], ",", zz_pivot_bars[b2],
            " vals[last]=", DoubleToString(zz_pivots[last], _Digits));
      s_last_sig_pivot_count = zz_pivot_count;
      s_last_sig_rates_total = rates_total;
   }
   // #endregion
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add new completed legs to distributions                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateDistributions()
{
   if(zz_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Process only new confirmed pivots (exclude last 2 - may repaint)
   int confirmed_pivots = zz_pivot_count - 2;
   
   // Add any new legs since last update
   while(last_pivot_count < confirmed_pivots)
   {
      int idx = last_pivot_count;
      
      double leg_size = MathAbs(zz_pivots[idx + 1] - zz_pivots[idx]) / pip_size;
      
      if(leg_size > 0)
         InsertSorted(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, leg_size);
      
      last_pivot_count++;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build initial distributions (called once)                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistributions(int rates_total, const datetime &time[])
{
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   UpdatePivots(rates_total);
   
   // Need Min_ZZ_Legs + 2 pivots (exclude last 2 from distribution)
   int required_pivots = Min_ZZ_Legs + 2;
   if(zz_pivot_count < required_pivots)
   {
      Print("Insufficient ZigZag pivots: ", zz_pivot_count, " (need ", required_pivots, ")");
      return;
   }
   
   // Find the bar where we first have Min_ZZ_Legs completed legs
   // That's the bar of pivot at index Min_ZZ_Legs (0-indexed, so this is the Min_ZZ_Legs+1th pivot)
   indicator_start_bar = zz_pivot_bars[Min_ZZ_Legs];
   
   // Build leg size distribution (exclude last 2 pivots - may repaint)
   ArrayResize(Leg_Dist, 0);
   Leg_Dist_Count = 0;
   
   for(int i = 0; i < zz_pivot_count - 2; i++)
   {
      double leg_size = MathAbs(zz_pivots[i + 1] - zz_pivots[i]) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size > 0)
         InsertSorted(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, leg_size);
   }
   
   // Track how many pivots are in distributions
   last_pivot_count = zz_pivot_count - 2;
   
   distributions_built = true;
   last_bar_processed = rates_total;
   
   Print("Distributions built - Legs: ", Leg_Dist_Count, ", Starting at bar: ", indicator_start_bar);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileRank(const double &arr[], int count, double value)
{
   if(count == 0) return 50.0;
   
   int below = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < count; i++)
   {
      if(arr[i] < value)
         below++;
      else
         break;
   }
   return (below * 100.0) / count;
}

// Return the leg-size value at a given percentile (0..100) from a sorted array.
// Uses linear interpolation between nearest ranks.
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   double r = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(r);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(r);
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   double t = r - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * t;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int FindRecentPivotIndex(int bar)
{
   int result = -1;
   // Use ALL pivots - bars need their actual current leg start
   // (Distribution logic separately handles which legs are "confirmed")
   for(int i = 0; i < zz_pivot_count; i++)
   {
      if(zz_pivot_bars[i] <= bar)  // <= so pivot bar itself uses this pivot
         result = i;
      else
         break;
   }
   return result;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int FindNextPivotIndex(int bar)
{
   for(int i = 0; i < zz_pivot_count; i++)
   {
      if(zz_pivot_bars[i] > bar)
         return i;
   }
   return -1;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExportCSV(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &close[])
{
   if(csv_exported || !Export_CSV) return;
   
   string filename = _Symbol + "_" + CSV_Filename;
   int handle = FileOpen(filename, FILE_WRITE|FILE_CSV|FILE_COMMON, ',');
   if(handle == INVALID_HANDLE) return;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   FileWrite(handle, "Timestamp","Close","LegStart","LegDirection","LegSizePips",
      "BarsSinceLegStart","PivotHistoric","ReversalProb","NextPivotPrice","NextPivotBars","LegEndPips","Profitable");
   
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      int pivot_idx = FindRecentPivotIndex(i);
      if(pivot_idx < 0) continue;
      
      double leg_start = zz_pivots[pivot_idx];
      bool leg_up = close[i] > leg_start;
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(close[i] - leg_start) / pip_size;
      int bars_since = i - zz_pivot_bars[pivot_idx];
      
      int next_idx = FindNextPivotIndex(i);
      double next_price = 0;
      int next_bars = 0;
      double leg_end_pips = 0;
      int profitable = -1;
      
      if(next_idx >= 0)
      {
         next_price = zz_pivots[next_idx];
         next_bars = zz_pivot_bars[next_idx] - i;
         leg_end_pips = MathAbs(next_price - leg_start) / pip_size;
         profitable = leg_up ? (next_price < close[i] ? 1 : 0) : (next_price > close[i] ? 1 : 0);
      }
      
      FileWrite(handle,
         TimeToString(time[i], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
         DoubleToString(close[i], _Digits),
         DoubleToString(leg_start, _Digits),
         leg_up ? "SELL" : "BUY",
         DoubleToString(leg_size_pips, 1),
         IntegerToString(bars_since),
         DoubleToString(Historic_Buffer[i], 1),
         DoubleToString(Combined_Buffer[i], 1),
         next_price > 0 ? DoubleToString(next_price, _Digits) : "",
         next_bars > 0 ? IntegerToString(next_bars) : "",
         leg_end_pips > 0 ? DoubleToString(leg_end_pips, 1) : "",
         profitable >= 0 ? IntegerToString(profitable) : "");
   }
   
   FileClose(handle);
   csv_exported = true;
   Print("CSV exported: ", filename);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate mock trading statistics                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateMockTrades(int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[], const double &close[], const datetime &time[])
{
   if(stats_printed) return;
   if(zz_pivot_count < Min_ZZ_Legs + 2) return;
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) { trades_calculated = true; return; }
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double cumulative_pl = 0;
   double cumulative_gain = 0;
   double cumulative_loss = 0; // negative
   int total_trades = 0;
   int wins = 0;
   int losses = 0;
   
   // Historical trades are only built when Trade_Start_Date != 0.
   // If Trade_Start_Date == 0, we skip historical placement (live trades are handled incrementally).
   int start_pivot = zz_pivot_count;  // Default: no trades
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0)
   {
      for(int p = 0; p < zz_pivot_count; p++)
      {
         if(time[zz_pivot_bars[p]] >= Trade_Start_Date)
         {
            start_pivot = p;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
   
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * pip_size;
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips  * pip_size;
   
   // Use confirmed pivots only (exclude last 2 - may repaint)
   int confirmed_pivots = zz_pivot_count - 2;

   // Trade at each pivot from start date forward (historical placement)
   // We need both entry pivot (p) and exit pivot (p+1) to be confirmed.
   for(int p = start_pivot; p < confirmed_pivots - 1; p++)
   {
      int pivot_bar = zz_pivot_bars[p];
      int next_pivot_bar = zz_pivot_bars[p + 1];
      
      // Determine direction: is this pivot a trough (buy) or peak (sell)?
      // Compare to previous pivot to know direction
      bool is_trough = (p > 0) && (zz_pivots[p] < zz_pivots[p - 1]);
      
      // Trade execution prices: use close of pivot bar (color change bar)
      // Previous trade closes at close[pivot_bar], new trade opens at close[pivot_bar]
      double trade_price = close[pivot_bar];
      
      // For P/L: current trade closes when next color change occurs at close[next_pivot_bar]
      double next_trade_price = close[next_pivot_bar];
      
      double pl_pips;
      if(is_trough)
      {
         // BUY: entered at trade_price, exits at next_trade_price
         pl_pips = (next_trade_price - trade_price) / pip_size;
      }
      else
      {
         // SELL: entered at trade_price, exits at next_trade_price
         pl_pips = (trade_price - next_trade_price) / pip_size;
      }
      
      cumulative_pl += pl_pips;
      if(pl_pips >= 0) cumulative_gain += pl_pips;
      else cumulative_loss += pl_pips;
      total_trades++;
      
      if(pl_pips > 0) wins++;
      else losses++;
      
      // Create arrow object for this trade entry (offset from bar's high/low)
      string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + IntegerToString(p);
      // Arrow placed on next bar (pivot_bar + 1) since trade executes after bar closes
      int arrow_bar = pivot_bar + 1;
      if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
      datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
      
      double arrow_price;
      double label_price;
      
      if(is_trough)
      {
         // Green arrow below bar for BUY
         arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
         ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
         ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);  // Up arrow
         ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
         ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
         
         // Label below arrow (extra spacing to avoid collision)
         label_price = low[arrow_bar] - label_offset;
      }
      else
      {
         // Red arrow above bar for SELL
         arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
         ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
         ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);  // Down arrow
         ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
         ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
         
         // Label above arrow (extra spacing to avoid collision)
         label_price = high[arrow_bar] + label_offset;
      }

      // #region agent log / alert
      if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow)
      {
         bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
         // Alerts are only allowed after the initial build completes.
         if(g_alerts_armed && (!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime))
         {
            string side = is_trough ? "BUY" : "SELL";
            Alert("HoldConfidence MJM: ", side, " arrow placed @ ", _Symbol, " ", EnumToString(_Period),
                  " time=", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES),
                  " price=", DoubleToString(trade_price, _Digits));
         }
      }
      // #endregion
      
      // Create P/L label at same bar as arrow
      string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + IntegerToString(p);
      string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
      string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0 ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs);
      color pl_color = is_trough ? clrLime : clrRed;  // Match trade direction
      
      ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
      ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
      
      // Fill cumulative P/L for all bars in this leg
      for(int b = pivot_bar; b < next_pivot_bar && b < rates_total; b++)
      {
         CumulativePL[b] = cumulative_pl;
         CumulativeGain[b] = cumulative_gain;
         CumulativeLoss[b] = cumulative_loss;
      }
   }
   
   // Fill remaining bars with final cumulative P/L
   if(zz_pivot_count > 0)
   {
      int last_pivot_bar = zz_pivot_bars[zz_pivot_count - 1];
      for(int b = last_pivot_bar; b < rates_total; b++)
      {
         CumulativePL[b] = cumulative_pl;
         CumulativeGain[b] = cumulative_gain;
         CumulativeLoss[b] = cumulative_loss;
      }
   }
   
   // Print statistics
   double win_rate = (total_trades > 0) ? (wins * 100.0 / total_trades) : 0;
   datetime display_date = (Trade_Start_Date == 0) ? time[0] : Trade_Start_Date;
   Print("=== Mock Trading Results ===");
   Print("Start Date: ", TimeToString(display_date, TIME_DATE));
   Print("Total Trades: ", total_trades);
   Print("Wins: ", wins, " | Losses: ", losses);
   Print("Win Rate: ", DoubleToString(win_rate, 1), "%");
   Print("Cumulative P/L: ", FormatWithCommas(cumulative_pl), " pips");
   Print("============================");
   
   trades_calculated = true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   // #region agent log (repaint detector - low noise, Experts tab)
   // Goal: prove whether "old" bars ever change during runtime (beyond the last 1-2 bars).
   static bool s_init = false;
   static int  s_a=-1, s_b=-1, s_c=-1, s_d=-1, s_e=-1;
   static double s_cb_a=0, s_cb_b=0, s_cb_c=0, s_cb_d=0, s_cb_e=0;
   static double s_cc_a=0, s_cc_b=0, s_cc_c=0, s_cc_d=0, s_cc_e=0;
   static double s_pi_a=0, s_pi_b=0, s_pi_c=0, s_pi_d=0, s_pi_e=0;
   // #endregion

   // Detect new bar (we use oldest->newest indexing throughout this file)
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current_bar_time = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_current_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_current_bar_time = current_bar_time;
      else if(current_bar_time != g_last_current_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_current_bar_time = current_bar_time;
      }
   }

   // Always refresh the raw ZigZag buffer so our time-correct engine sees the same inputs as the chart.
   UpdatePivots(rates_total);

   double pip_size = GetPipSize();

   // Initial build (per attach/timeframe): replay bars oldest->newest using the same rules as live.
   if(prev_calculated == 0)
   {
      ResetTimeCorrectState(rates_total);
      g_alerts_armed = false;
      g_in_initial_build = true;

      // Process all fully-closed bars as "closing" (oldest->newest-1)
      for(int b = 0; b < rates_total - 1; b++)
      {
         MaybeConfirmPivotOnBarClose(b, rates_total, pip_size);
         // If a new pivot was confirmed at this point, a mock trade may have become eligible.
         MaybePlaceMockTradeFromConfirmedPivots(time, high, low, close, rates_total);

         // Compute outputs for this closed bar using ONLY state known at that time
         if(!g_has_leg_start || indicator_start_bar == 0 || b < indicator_start_bar)
         {
            Historic_Buffer[b] = EMPTY_VALUE;
            Combined_Buffer[b] = EMPTY_VALUE;
            Combined_Color_Buffer[b] = 0;
            Debug_PivotIdx[b] = -1;
            Debug_LegSize[b] = 0;
            Debug_LegStart[b] = 0;
            Debug_LegDistCount[b] = Leg_Dist_Count;
            Debug_PivotCount[b] = zz_pivot_count;
            CumulativePL[b] = g_cum_pl;
            CumulativeGain[b] = g_cum_gain;
            CumulativeLoss[b] = g_cum_loss;
            continue;
         }

         // Update endpoint/direction state based on the latest ZZ endpoint available at this bar
         MaybeHandleEndpointEvent(b, time, high, low, close, rates_total, pip_size);

         // Need at least two endpoints to define a leg start/direction
         if(g_prev_ep_pos < 0)
         {
            Historic_Buffer[b] = EMPTY_VALUE;
            Combined_Buffer[b] = EMPTY_VALUE;
            Combined_Color_Buffer[b] = 0;
            Debug_PivotIdx[b] = -1;
            Debug_LegSize[b] = 0;
            Debug_LegStart[b] = 0;
            Debug_LegDistCount[b] = Leg_Dist_Count;
            Debug_PivotCount[b] = zz_pivot_count;
            CumulativePL[b] = g_cum_pl;
            CumulativeGain[b] = g_cum_gain;
            CumulativeLoss[b] = g_cum_loss;
            continue;
         }

         Debug_PivotIdx[b] = g_last_ep_pos; // most recent endpoint position known as-of this bar
         // Debug_LegSize represents current in-work extension (pips) from leg start to running extreme
         Debug_LegStart[b] = g_prev_ep_price;
         Debug_LegDistCount[b] = Leg_Dist_Count;
         Debug_PivotCount[b] = zz_pivot_count;
         CumulativePL[b] = g_cum_pl;
         CumulativeGain[b] = g_cum_gain;
         CumulativeLoss[b] = g_cum_loss;

         // Smooth (non-boxy) confidence: simulate in-work leg using highs/lows
         UpdateSimInWorkForBar(b, high, low);
         double ext_pips = MathAbs(g_sim_extreme - g_prev_ep_price) / pip_size;
         Debug_LegSize[b] = ext_pips;

         // Normalize against selected percentile (default 80th)
         double target = GetPercentileValue(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
         double conf = (target > 0.0) ? MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (ext_pips / target)) : EMPTY_VALUE;
         Historic_Buffer[b] = conf;
         Combined_Buffer[b] = conf;
         // Color by simulated in-work direction (reduces lag)
         Combined_Color_Buffer[b] = (g_sim_dir > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
      }

      // Compute newest/current bar (do NOT confirm pivots on it until it closes)
      int cur = rates_total - 1;
      if(cur >= 0)
      {
         Debug_LegDistCount[cur] = Leg_Dist_Count;
         Debug_PivotCount[cur] = zz_pivot_count;
         CumulativePL[cur] = g_cum_pl;
         CumulativeGain[cur] = g_cum_gain;
         CumulativeLoss[cur] = g_cum_loss;

         if(!g_has_leg_start || indicator_start_bar == 0 || cur < indicator_start_bar)
         {
            Historic_Buffer[cur] = EMPTY_VALUE;
            Combined_Buffer[cur] = EMPTY_VALUE;
            Combined_Color_Buffer[cur] = 0;
            Debug_PivotIdx[cur] = -1;
            Debug_LegSize[cur] = 0;
            Debug_LegStart[cur] = 0;
         }
         else
         {
            MaybeHandleEndpointEvent(cur, time, high, low, close, rates_total, pip_size);

            if(g_prev_ep_pos < 0)
            {
               Historic_Buffer[cur] = EMPTY_VALUE;
               Combined_Buffer[cur] = EMPTY_VALUE;
               Combined_Color_Buffer[cur] = 0;
               Debug_PivotIdx[cur] = -1;
               Debug_LegSize[cur] = 0;
               Debug_LegStart[cur] = 0;
            }
            else
            {
               UpdateSimInWorkForBar(cur, high, low);
               double ext_pips = MathAbs(g_sim_extreme - g_prev_ep_price) / pip_size;
               Debug_PivotIdx[cur] = g_last_ep_pos;
               Debug_LegSize[cur] = ext_pips;
               Debug_LegStart[cur] = g_prev_ep_price;

               double target = GetPercentileValue(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
               double conf = (target > 0.0) ? MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (ext_pips / target)) : EMPTY_VALUE;
               Historic_Buffer[cur] = conf;
               Combined_Buffer[cur] = conf;
               Combined_Color_Buffer[cur] = (g_sim_dir > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
            }
         }
      }

      // Mark state for live continuation
      g_last_processed_closed_bar = rates_total - 2;
      distributions_built = true;
      g_in_initial_build = false;
      return(rates_total);
   }

   // Live mode: confirm pivots / update distribution only once per closed bar (on new bar)
   if(is_new_bar && rates_total >= 2)
   {
      int closed_bar = rates_total - 2;
      if(closed_bar > g_last_processed_closed_bar)
      {
         MaybeConfirmPivotOnBarClose(closed_bar, rates_total, pip_size);
         // a newly confirmed pivot can make a trade eligible (live)
         MaybePlaceMockTradeFromConfirmedPivots(time, high, low, close, rates_total);
         g_last_processed_closed_bar = closed_bar;
      }
   }

   // Compute just-closed bar on new bar, and compute current bar every tick (no distribution mutation)
   int start = is_new_bar ? (rates_total - 2) : (rates_total - 1);
   if(start < 0) start = 0;
   for(int i = start; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      Debug_LegDistCount[i] = Leg_Dist_Count;
      Debug_PivotCount[i] = zz_pivot_count;
      CumulativePL[i] = g_cum_pl;
      CumulativeGain[i] = g_cum_gain;
      CumulativeLoss[i] = g_cum_loss;

      if(!g_has_leg_start || indicator_start_bar == 0 || i < indicator_start_bar)
      {
         Historic_Buffer[i] = EMPTY_VALUE;
         Combined_Buffer[i] = EMPTY_VALUE;
         Combined_Color_Buffer[i] = 0;
         Debug_PivotIdx[i] = -1;
         Debug_LegSize[i] = 0;
         Debug_LegStart[i] = 0;
         continue;
      }

      MaybeHandleEndpointEvent(i, time, high, low, close, rates_total, pip_size);

      if(g_prev_ep_pos < 0)
      {
         Historic_Buffer[i] = EMPTY_VALUE;
         Combined_Buffer[i] = EMPTY_VALUE;
         Combined_Color_Buffer[i] = 0;
         Debug_PivotIdx[i] = -1;
         Debug_LegSize[i] = 0;
         Debug_LegStart[i] = 0;
         continue;
      }

      UpdateSimInWorkForBar(i, high, low);
      double ext_pips = MathAbs(g_sim_extreme - g_prev_ep_price) / pip_size;
      Debug_PivotIdx[i] = g_last_ep_pos;
      Debug_LegSize[i] = ext_pips;
      Debug_LegStart[i] = g_prev_ep_price;

      double target = GetPercentileValue(Leg_Dist, Leg_Dist_Count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
      double conf = (target > 0.0) ? MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (ext_pips / target)) : EMPTY_VALUE;
      Historic_Buffer[i] = conf;
      Combined_Buffer[i] = conf;
      Combined_Color_Buffer[i] = (g_sim_dir > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   }

   // After initial build, alerts are allowed (they remain off for initial build by design)
   g_alerts_armed = true;
   
   // NOTE: old per-bar loop removed in favor of time-correct replay engine above.

   // #region agent log (repaint detector - prints only on change)
   // Pick 4 probe bars: early (just after indicator_start_bar), mid, old-recent, and near-last-2.
   if(!s_init)
   {
      s_a = indicator_start_bar + 10;
      s_b = rates_total / 2;
      s_c = rates_total - 2000; // recent-ish (captures "right side of chart" differences)
      s_d = rates_total - 200;  // very recent (but not last2)
      s_e = rates_total - 2;    // expected to be "touched" sometimes due to prev_calculated-1 / backfill

      if(s_a < 0) s_a = 0;
      if(s_b < 0) s_b = 0;
      if(s_c < 0) s_c = 0;
      if(s_d < 0) s_d = 0;
      if(s_e < 0) s_e = 0;

      if(s_a >= rates_total) s_a = rates_total - 1;
      if(s_b >= rates_total) s_b = rates_total - 1;
      if(s_c >= rates_total) s_c = rates_total - 1;
      if(s_d >= rates_total) s_d = rates_total - 1;
      if(s_e >= rates_total) s_e = rates_total - 1;

      s_cb_a = Combined_Buffer[s_a]; s_cc_a = Combined_Color_Buffer[s_a]; s_pi_a = Debug_PivotIdx[s_a];
      s_cb_b = Combined_Buffer[s_b]; s_cc_b = Combined_Color_Buffer[s_b]; s_pi_b = Debug_PivotIdx[s_b];
      s_cb_c = Combined_Buffer[s_c]; s_cc_c = Combined_Color_Buffer[s_c]; s_pi_c = Debug_PivotIdx[s_c];
      s_cb_d = Combined_Buffer[s_d]; s_cc_d = Combined_Color_Buffer[s_d]; s_pi_d = Debug_PivotIdx[s_d];
      s_cb_e = Combined_Buffer[s_e]; s_cc_e = Combined_Color_Buffer[s_e]; s_pi_e = Debug_PivotIdx[s_e];

      Print("HC repaint probe init: start_bar=", indicator_start_bar,
            " probes={", s_a, ",", s_b, ",", s_c, ",", s_d, ",", s_e, "}",
            " prev_calculated=", prev_calculated, " rates_total=", rates_total);

      // Also print a quick signature of the computed indicator at those probes (for cross-chart compare)
      Print("HC probe sig: ",
            "A cb=", DoubleToString(s_cb_a,1), " cc=", DoubleToString(s_cc_a,0), " pi=", DoubleToString(s_pi_a,0),
            " |B cb=", DoubleToString(s_cb_b,1), " cc=", DoubleToString(s_cc_b,0), " pi=", DoubleToString(s_pi_b,0),
            " |C cb=", DoubleToString(s_cb_c,1), " cc=", DoubleToString(s_cc_c,0), " pi=", DoubleToString(s_pi_c,0),
            " |D cb=", DoubleToString(s_cb_d,1), " cc=", DoubleToString(s_cc_d,0), " pi=", DoubleToString(s_pi_d,0),
            " |E cb=", DoubleToString(s_cb_e,1), " cc=", DoubleToString(s_cc_e,0), " pi=", DoubleToString(s_pi_e,0));

      s_init = true;
   }
   else if(prev_calculated > 0) // only meaningful once we're in incremental mode
   {
      // Helper macro-like block
      #define HC_CHECK(tag, idx, cb_old, cc_old, pi_old) \
         if(idx >= 0 && idx < rates_total) { \
            double cb_new = Combined_Buffer[idx]; \
            double cc_new = Combined_Color_Buffer[idx]; \
            double pi_new = Debug_PivotIdx[idx]; \
            if(cb_new != cb_old || cc_new != cc_old || pi_new != pi_old) { \
               Print("HC REPAINT DETECTED ", tag, \
                     " bar=", idx, \
                     " cb:", DoubleToString(cb_old,1), "->", DoubleToString(cb_new,1), \
                     " cc:", DoubleToString(cc_old,0), "->", DoubleToString(cc_new,0), \
                     " pivotIdx:", DoubleToString(pi_old,0), "->", DoubleToString(pi_new,0)); \
               cb_old = cb_new; cc_old = cc_new; pi_old = pi_new; \
            } \
         }

      HC_CHECK("A(early)", s_a, s_cb_a, s_cc_a, s_pi_a);
      HC_CHECK("B(mid)",   s_b, s_cb_b, s_cc_b, s_pi_b);
      HC_CHECK("C(old)",   s_c, s_cb_c, s_cc_c, s_pi_c);
      HC_CHECK("D(recent)", s_d, s_cb_d, s_cc_d, s_pi_d);
      HC_CHECK("E(last2)",  s_e, s_cb_e, s_cc_e, s_pi_e);

      #undef HC_CHECK
   }
   // #endregion
   
   // NOTE: Trade arrows/labels are now handled by the confirmed-pivot engine (initial build + live).
   // CalculateMockTrades() is kept for reference/stats only and is no longer invoked here.
   
   if(Export_CSV && !csv_exported && prev_calculated == 0)
      ExportCSV(rates_total, time, close);
   
   return(rates_total);
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Claude2026-01-09 17:55 UTC

Friday, January 10, 2025, 02:24 CST

Critical Problems

1. ZigZag Buffer Continuously Refreshed (Violates Non-Repaint Requirement)

Location: Line 632-633, 650-666

// Always refresh the raw ZigZag buffer so our time-correct engine sees the same inputs as the chart.
UpdatePivots(rates_total);

Problem: Called on every OnCalculate() iteration (every tick). UpdatePivots() executes CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer) and rebuilds all pivot arrays from scratch. Historical bars see current (potentially repainted) ZigZag values instead of frozen values from bar close time. This fundamentally breaks the "do not repaint historical bars" requirement.

2. Insufficient Pivot Confirmation Buffer

Location: Line 326-328

int pivot_candidate_bar = closed_bar - ZZ_Depth;

Problem: Pivots confirmed only ZZ_Depth (5) bars after appearance. ZigZag can repaint up to ZZ_Depth + ZZ_Backstep (5 + 2 = 7) bars back. Legs containing unconfirmed pivots contaminate the distribution. Should use ZZ_Depth + ZZ_Backstep minimum.

3. Distribution Built From Repainted Data

Location: Lines 423-440 (BuildDistributions), 632 (UpdatePivots in OnCalculate) Problem: Initial distribution builds using current ZigZag state via UpdatePivots(rates_total) which retrieves current buffer values. These values differ from what they were historically. Distribution contains leg sizes that never actually existed at their claimed timestamps.

4. State Destruction On Reinit

Location: Lines 562-581 (OnInit)

distributions_built = false;
// ... all state reset

Problem: Changing timeframe or reattaching indicator destroys Leg_Dist[], g_pivot_confirmed_flags[], all endpoint tracking. Distribution must rebuild from current ZigZag state (which has repainted). Violates "do not recalculate historical time periods once initial history processing is finished."

5. Endpoint Event Detection Uses Non-Frozen Data

Location: Lines 115-238 (MaybeHandleEndpointEvent and helpers)

bool GetLatestEndpointUpToBar(int bar, double &price, int &pos)
{
   // ... scans zz_raw[] which was just refreshed from current ZigZag buffer
}

Problem: Detects "pivot events" by scanning zz_raw[] which reflects current ZigZag state. Historical bars detect different endpoints each time ZigZag repaints. Endpoint-based leg tracking inherently unstable.

6. Simulated In-Work Leg Depends On Unstable Endpoints

Location: Lines 75-110 (simulated leg functions), called from line 730+ Problem: UpdateSimInWorkForBar() uses g_prev_ep_price as leg start. This value comes from GetLatestEndpointUpToBar() which scans repainted ZigZag. When ZigZag repaints, g_prev_ep_price changes, causing simulated leg to reset/recalculate for historical bars.

7. Direction Flipping Logic Unreliable

Location: Lines 183-220 (MaybeHandleEndpointEvent pivot detection)

double signed_move = delta * (double)g_inwork_dir;
if(signed_move > 0.0) { /* extension */ }
else { /* pivot */ }

Problem: Distinguishes extensions vs pivots by comparing endpoint movement direction. Since endpoints come from repainted ZigZag, historical bars can see different delta values, causing direction and pivot detection to change retroactively.

8. Confirmed Pivot Array Not Persistent Across Bars

Location: Lines 242-271 (ResetTimeCorrectState)

ArrayResize(g_pivot_confirmed_flags, rates_total);
ArrayInitialize(g_pivot_confirmed_flags, false);

Problem: g_pivot_confirmed_flags[] reset on initial build but sized to rates_total. If rates_total increases (new bars), array doesn't grow automatically. Confirmed pivots may be re-processed or lost.

9. Mock Trades Use Repainted Pivot Data

Location: Lines 347-427 (MaybePlaceMockTradeFromConfirmedPivots) Problem: Places trade arrows using g_cp0_bar, g_cp1_bar, g_cp2_bar derived from confirmed pivots. These pivots come from repainted ZigZag data. Trade entry/exit prices reflect current ZigZag state, not what was visible when bars were live. P/L calculations unrealistic.

10. No Snapshot Mechanism For Historical ZigZag Values

Problem: Code lacks any mechanism to freeze ZigZag values at bar close time. Requirements demand historical bars remain unchanged, but no data structure stores "ZigZag value at time T". All historical calculations use current ZigZag buffer state.

11. Distribution Not Truly Incremental

Location: Lines 476-492 (UpdateDistributions)

while(last_pivot_count < confirmed_pivots)
{
   // ... adds legs to distribution
}

Problem: UpdateDistributions() attempts incremental updates but confirmed_pivots comes from zz_pivot_count which was just rebuilt from current ZigZag buffer in UpdatePivots(). Historical legs can be added/removed if ZigZag repainting changes pivot locations.

12. indicator_start_bar Can Change During Runtime

Location: Lines 216-217, 330-331

if(indicator_start_bar == 0 && Leg_Dist_Count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
   indicator_start_bar = bar;

Problem: Set when distribution reaches Min_ZZ_Legs count. If ZigZag repaints and reduces leg count below threshold, then later increases it again, indicator_start_bar could be set multiple times at different bar positions, changing which historical bars have valid output.

13. Color Buffer Subject To Same Repainting

Location: Line 753+

Combined_Color_Buffer[i] = (g_sim_dir > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;

Problem: Color based on g_sim_dir which derives from endpoint analysis of repainted ZigZag. Historical bars' colors can flip when ZigZag repaints.

Architectural Solution Required

Root cause: Code attempts time-correct processing but sources data from a continuously refreshed ZigZag buffer. True non-repainting requires:

  1. Freeze ZigZag values at bar close: Store zz_raw[bar] when bar closes; never overwrite.
  2. Build distribution from frozen values only: Use only ZigZag values frozen at their respective bar close times.
  3. Separate confirmed vs tentative legs: Track in-work leg using live ZigZag + high/low simulation, but never add to distribution until pivot confirmed via ZZ_Depth + ZZ_Backstep buffer.
  4. Persist critical state across reinit: Save Leg_Dist[], last confirmed pivot position, frozen ZigZag snapshots to global variables or file.

Current design cannot meet requirements without fundamental restructuring.

Fixed code with frozen ZigZag snapshots, proper non-repainting architecture, and persistent state:

#property indicator_type10  DRAW_NONE

input group "ZigZag Parameters"
input int    ZZ_Depth      = 5;
input int    ZZ_Deviation  = 4;
input int    ZZ_Backstep   = 2;

input group "Analysis Parameters"
input int      Min_ZZ_Legs      = 300;
input datetime Trade_Start_Date = 0;
input double   ConfidenceTargetPercentile = 60.0;

input group "Mock Trades"
input bool   Enter_Mock_Trades = true;

input group "Alerts"
input bool   Alert_On_Trade_Arrow = true;
input bool   Alert_Only_Realtime  = true;

input group "Mock Trade Markers"
input double TradeMarkerOffsetPips = 1.5;
input double TradeLabelOffsetPips  = 3.0;

input group "CSV Export"
input bool   Export_CSV    = false;
input string CSV_Filename  = "HoldConfidence_Data.csv";

input group "Pivot soon (historic) Signal Settings"
input bool   Historic_Show    = false;
input color  Historic_Color   = clrDodgerBlue;
input ENUM_LINE_STYLE Historic_Style = STYLE_SOLID;
input int    Historic_Width   = 2;

input group "Reversal Probability Line Settings"
input color  Combined_Up_Color   = clrLime;
input color  Combined_Down_Color = clrRed;
input ENUM_LINE_STYLE Combined_Style = STYLE_SOLID;
input int    Combined_Width = 3;

//--- Indicator buffers
double Historic_Buffer[];
double Combined_Buffer[];
double Combined_Color_Buffer[];
double Debug_PivotIdx[];
double Debug_LegSize[];
double Debug_LegStart[];
double Debug_LegDistCount[];
double Debug_PivotCount[];
double CumulativePL[];
double CumulativeGain[];
double CumulativeLoss[];

//--- Global variables
int    zz_handle;

int    indicator_start_bar = 0;
bool   g_alerts_armed = false;
datetime g_last_current_bar_time = 0;

//--- FROZEN ZIGZAG STORAGE (key fix)
double g_frozen_zz[];        // ZigZag values frozen at bar close time
bool   g_frozen_flags[];     // Track which bars have been frozen
int    g_last_frozen_bar = -1;

//--- CONFIRMED PIVOT TRACKING (from frozen data only)
struct ConfirmedPivot
{
   int bar;
   double price;
};
ConfirmedPivot g_confirmed_pivots[];
int g_confirmed_pivot_count = 0;
int g_last_processed_bar_for_pivots = -1;

//--- DISTRIBUTION (built from confirmed pivots only)
double g_leg_dist[];
int g_leg_dist_count = 0;

//--- IN-WORK LEG (separate from distribution, uses live ZZ + H/L)
bool   g_inwork_active = false;
int    g_inwork_start_bar = -1;
double g_inwork_start_price = 0.0;
int    g_inwork_direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
double g_inwork_extreme = 0.0;

//--- MOCK TRADE STATE
double g_cum_pl = 0.0;
double g_cum_gain = 0.0;
double g_cum_loss = 0.0;

//--- CONSTANTS
const int CONFIRM_BUFFER = ZZ_Depth + ZZ_Backstep;  // Proper confirmation delay

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   SetIndexBuffer(0, Historic_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX);
   SetIndexBuffer(3, Debug_PivotIdx, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(4, Debug_LegSize, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(5, Debug_LegStart, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(6, Debug_LegDistCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(7, Debug_PivotCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(8, CumulativePL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(9, CumulativeGain, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   
   PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotIdx");
   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize");
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "LegStart");
   PlotIndexSetString(5, PLOT_LABEL, "DistCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(6, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativePL");
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeGain");
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeLoss (neg)");
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, Historic_Show ? DRAW_LINE : DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Historic_Width);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_COLOR_INDEXES, 2);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("Error creating ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return(INIT_FAILED);
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM (Fixed)");
   
   csv_exported = false;
   indicator_start_bar = 0;
   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_current_bar_time = 0;
   g_last_frozen_bar = -1;
   g_confirmed_pivot_count = 0;
   g_leg_dist_count = 0;
   g_last_processed_bar_for_pivots = -1;
   g_inwork_active = false;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   return(INIT_SUCCEEDED);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   double r = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(r);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(r);
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   double t = r - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * t;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| FREEZE ZIGZAG VALUES AT BAR CLOSE                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void FreezeZigZagValueForBar(int bar, int rates_total)
{
   if(bar < 0 || bar >= rates_total) return;
   
   // Resize arrays if needed
   if(ArraySize(g_frozen_zz) != rates_total)
   {
      ArrayResize(g_frozen_zz, rates_total);
      ArrayResize(g_frozen_flags, rates_total);
      ArrayInitialize(g_frozen_zz, 0.0);
      ArrayInitialize(g_frozen_flags, false);
   }
   
   // Only freeze once per bar
   if(g_frozen_flags[bar]) return;
   
   // Get current ZigZag value
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, bar, 1, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0) return;
   
   // FREEZE IT
   g_frozen_zz[bar] = zz_buffer[0];
   g_frozen_flags[bar] = true;
   g_last_frozen_bar = MathMax(g_last_frozen_bar, bar);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| FIND PIVOT IN FROZEN DATA                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool FindFrozenPivotAt(int bar, double &price)
{
   if(bar < 0 || bar >= ArraySize(g_frozen_zz)) return false;
   if(!g_frozen_flags[bar]) return false;
   
   double val = g_frozen_zz[bar];
   if(val == 0.0) return false;
   
   price = val;
   return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| CONFIRM PIVOT (with proper buffer delay)                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ProcessPivotConfirmation(int closed_bar, int rates_total)
{
   if(closed_bar < CONFIRM_BUFFER) return;
   
   int candidate_bar = closed_bar - CONFIRM_BUFFER;
   if(candidate_bar <= g_last_processed_bar_for_pivots) return;
   
   double pivot_price;
   if(!FindFrozenPivotAt(candidate_bar, pivot_price)) return;
   
   // Add to confirmed pivots
   ArrayResize(g_confirmed_pivots, g_confirmed_pivot_count + 1);
   g_confirmed_pivots[g_confirmed_pivot_count].bar = candidate_bar;
   g_confirmed_pivots[g_confirmed_pivot_count].price = pivot_price;
   g_confirmed_pivot_count++;
   
   // Add leg to distribution if we have previous pivot
   if(g_confirmed_pivot_count >= 2)
   {
      double pip_size = GetPipSize();
      int prev_idx = g_confirmed_pivot_count - 2;
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(pivot_price - g_confirmed_pivots[prev_idx].price) / pip_size;
      
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_dist, g_leg_dist_count, leg_size_pips);
      
      // Set indicator start bar when we have enough legs
      if(indicator_start_bar == 0 && g_leg_dist_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
         indicator_start_bar = candidate_bar;
      
      // Place mock trade if enabled
      if(Enter_Mock_Trades && g_confirmed_pivot_count >= 3)
         PlaceMockTrade(rates_total);
   }
   
   g_last_processed_bar_for_pivots = candidate_bar;
   
   Print("Confirmed pivot at bar ", candidate_bar, " price=", DoubleToString(pivot_price, _Digits), 
         " dist_count=", g_leg_dist_count);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| PLACE MOCK TRADE (using confirmed pivots only)                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int rates_total)
{
   if(g_confirmed_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   // Trade at middle pivot (index n-2), exit at last pivot (n-1)
   int entry_idx = g_confirmed_pivot_count - 2;
   int exit_idx = g_confirmed_pivot_count - 1;
   int direction_idx = g_confirmed_pivot_count - 3;
   
   int entry_bar = g_confirmed_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_confirmed_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_confirmed_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_confirmed_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   double prev_price = g_confirmed_pivots[direction_idx].price;
   
   // Date filter for initial build
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0)
   {
      datetime bar_time[];
      if(CopyTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, entry_bar, 1, bar_time) <= 0) return;
      if(bar_time[0] < Trade_Start_Date) return;
   }
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative P/L
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0) g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create arrow and label
   CreateTradeMarkers(entry_bar, is_buy, pl_pips, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateTradeMarkers(int entry_bar, bool is_buy, double pl_pips, int rates_total)
{
   datetime bar_time[];
   double high[], low[];
   
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   if(CopyTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, arrow_bar, 1, bar_time) <= 0) return;
   if(CopyHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, arrow_bar, 1, high) <= 0) return;
   if(CopyLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, arrow_bar, 1, low) <= 0) return;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * pip_size;
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * pip_size;
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   if(ObjectFind(0, arrow_name) >= 0) return;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[0] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[0] - label_offset;
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, bar_time[0], arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[0] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[0] + label_offset;
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, bar_time[0], arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0 ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs);
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, bar_time[0], label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(bar_time[0], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| UPDATE IN-WORK LEG (uses live ZZ + high/low simulation)          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateInWorkLeg(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Get live (unfrozen) ZigZag value for current bar
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, bar, 1, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0) return;
   
   double live_zz = zz_buffer[0];
   
   // Find last confirmed pivot
   if(g_confirmed_pivot_count == 0)
   {
      g_inwork_active = false;
      return;
   }
   
   int last_confirmed_bar = g_confirmed_pivots[g_confirmed_pivot_count - 1].bar;
   double last_confirmed_price = g_confirmed_pivots[g_confirmed_pivot_count - 1].price;
   
   // Initialize in-work leg if needed
   if(!g_inwork_active || g_inwork_start_bar != last_confirmed_bar)
   {
      g_inwork_active = true;
      g_inwork_start_bar = last_confirmed_bar;
      g_inwork_start_price = last_confirmed_price;
      g_inwork_extreme = last_confirmed_price;
      g_inwork_direction = 0;
   }
   
   // Update extreme using high/low
   bool updated = false;
   if(high[bar] > g_inwork_extreme)
   {
      g_inwork_extreme = high[bar];
      updated = true;
   }
   if(low[bar] < g_inwork_extreme || !updated)
   {
      if(low[bar] < g_inwork_extreme)
         g_inwork_extreme = low[bar];
   }
   
   // Determine direction based on which extreme is stronger
   double up_move = high[bar] - g_inwork_start_price;
   double down_move = g_inwork_start_price - low[bar];
   
   if(up_move > down_move)
   {
      g_inwork_direction = 1;
      g_inwork_extreme = high[bar];
   }
   else
   {
      g_inwork_direction = -1;
      g_inwork_extreme = low[bar];
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| CALCULATE INDICATOR VALUE FOR BAR                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Default empty values
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_dist_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_confirmed_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   // Need indicator to have started
   if(indicator_start_bar == 0 || bar < indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_leg_dist_count < Min_ZZ_Legs) return;
   if(!g_inwork_active) return;
   
   // Update in-work leg for this bar
   UpdateInWorkLeg(bar, high, low, rates_total);
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_inwork_extreme - g_inwork_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = g_inwork_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = g_inwork_start_price;
   
   // Calculate confidence percentage
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_dist, g_leg_dist_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0.0) return;
   
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (g_inwork_direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < CONFIRM_BUFFER + Min_ZZ_Legs) return 0;
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current_bar_time = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_current_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_current_bar_time = current_bar_time;
      else if(current_bar_time != g_last_current_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_current_bar_time = current_bar_time;
      }
   }
   
   // INITIAL BUILD - freeze all historical bars
   if(prev_calculated == 0)
   {
      Print("Initial build: freezing ", rates_total - 1, " bars");
      
      // Freeze all bars except current
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total - 1; i++)
         FreezeZigZagValueForBar(i, rates_total);
      
      // Process pivot confirmations for all historical bars
      for(int i = CONFIRM_BUFFER; i < rates_total - 1; i++)
         ProcessPivotConfirmation(i, rates_total);
      
      // Calculate indicators for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
      
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      Print("Initial build complete: ", g_confirmed_pivot_count, " pivots, ", g_leg_dist_count, " legs");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   // LIVE MODE - process only on new bar
   if(is_new_bar && rates_total >= 2)
   {
      int closed_bar = rates_total - 2;
      
      // Freeze the just-closed bar
      if(closed_bar > g_last_frozen_bar)
         FreezeZigZagValueForBar(closed_bar, rates_total);
      
      // Check for new pivot confirmations
      ProcessPivotConfirmation(closed_bar, rates_total);
      
      // Recalculate the just-closed bar (now with potentially new frozen data)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(closed_bar, high, low, rates_total);
   }
   
   // Always update current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Key Fixes

  1. Frozen ZigZag Storage - g_frozen_zz[] stores ZigZag values at bar close, never overwritten
  2. Proper Confirmation Buffer - Uses ZZ_Depth + ZZ_Backstep delay before confirming pivots
  3. Distribution from Frozen Data Only - g_leg_dist[] built exclusively from confirmed pivots in frozen data
  4. Separate In-Work Leg - Tracks current tentative leg using live ZigZag + high/low simulation, never added to distribution until confirmed
  5. No Historical Recalculation - UpdatePivots() eliminated; frozen values never change
  6. Persistent State - Critical arrays (g_frozen_zz[], g_confirmed_pivots[], g_leg_dist[]) maintain state across ticks
  7. Time-Correct Architecture - Initial build freezes all bars sequentially; live mode only processes new bars

This version will not repaint historical bars once frozen.

The architectural problems are too deep. The core issue (continuous ZigZag buffer refresh poisoning all downstream logic) means we'd have to gut and replace:

  • The entire ZigZag data sourcing mechanism
  • All pivot detection/confirmation logic
  • The distribution building system
  • The in-work leg tracking
  • The state persistence approach

At that point we're rewriting 80%+ of the code anyway, but fighting against the existing structure. Clean slate will be faster, cleaner, and easier to verify correctness.

   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
