   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);

   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);

   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize");
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "LegStart");
   PlotIndexSetString(5, PLOT_LABEL, "DistCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(6, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativePL");
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeGain");
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeLoss (neg)");

   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);

   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM v8");
   
   // Initialize state
   g_last_frozen_bar = -1;
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   g_leg_count = 0;
   g_distribution_ready = false;
   g_indicator_start_bar = 0;
   g_inwork_active = false;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot = -1;
   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_bar_time = 0;
   g_csv_exported = false;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   Print("HoldConfidence initialized - ZZ Depth:", ZZ_Depth, " Backstep:", ZZ_Backstep, " Confirm delay:", CONFIRM_DELAY);
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| LAYER 1: FREEZE ZIGZAG AT BAR CLOSE                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void FreezeBarAtClose(int bar, int rates_total)
{
   if(bar < 0 || bar >= rates_total) return;
   
   // Resize arrays if needed
   if(ArraySize(g_frozen_zz) < rates_total)
   {
      ArrayResize(g_frozen_zz, rates_total);
      ArrayResize(g_bar_frozen, rates_total);
      for(int i = ArraySize(g_frozen_zz) - rates_total; i < 0; i++)
      {
         g_frozen_zz[i] = 0.0;
         g_bar_frozen[i] = false;
      }
   }
   
   // Only freeze once per bar
   if(g_bar_frozen[bar]) return;
   
   // Copy current ZigZag value
   double zz_temp[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_temp, false);
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, bar, 1, zz_temp);
   
   if(copied > 0)
   {
      g_frozen_zz[bar] = zz_temp[0];
      g_bar_frozen[bar] = true;
      g_last_frozen_bar = MathMax(g_last_frozen_bar, bar);
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| LAYER 2: CONFIRM PIVOTS FROM FROZEN DATA                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForNewConfirmedPivots(int closed_bar, int rates_total, const datetime &time[])
{
   if(closed_bar < CONFIRM_DELAY) return;
   
   int candidate_bar = closed_bar - CONFIRM_DELAY;
   
   // Check if this bar has a pivot in frozen data
   if(candidate_bar < 0 || candidate_bar >= ArraySize(g_frozen_zz)) return;
   if(!g_bar_frozen[candidate_bar]) return;
   
   double pivot_value = g_frozen_zz[candidate_bar];
   if(pivot_value == 0.0) return;
   
   // Check if already confirmed
   for(int i = 0; i < g_pivot_count; i++)
   {
      if(g_pivots[i].bar == candidate_bar)
         return;  // Already confirmed
   }
   
   // Add to confirmed pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
   g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = candidate_bar;
   g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = pivot_value;
   g_pivot_count++;
   
   Print("Pivot confirmed: bar=", candidate_bar, " price=", DoubleToString(pivot_value, _Digits), 
         " total_pivots=", g_pivot_count);
   
   // Add leg to distribution if we have 2+ pivots
   if(g_pivot_count >= 2)
   {
      AddLegToDistribution(g_pivot_count - 2, g_pivot_count - 1);
   }
   
   // Check for mock trade opportunity (need 3+ pivots)
   if(Enter_Mock_Trades && g_pivot_count >= 3)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(g_pivot_count - 2, rates_total, time);
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| LAYER 3: ADD LEG TO DISTRIBUTION                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddLegToDistribution(int pivot_idx1, int pivot_idx2)
{
   if(pivot_idx1 < 0 || pivot_idx2 < 0) return;
   if(pivot_idx1 >= g_pivot_count || pivot_idx2 >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   double price1 = g_pivots[pivot_idx1].price;
   double price2 = g_pivots[pivot_idx2].price;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(price2 - price1) / pip_size;
   
   if(leg_size_pips > 0)
   {
      InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
      
      Print("Leg added: size=", DoubleToString(leg_size_pips, 1), " pips, total_legs=", g_leg_count);
      
      // Check if distribution ready
      if(!g_distribution_ready && g_leg_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
      {
         g_distribution_ready = true;
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[pivot_idx2].bar;
         Print("Distribution ready! Start bar: ", g_indicator_start_bar);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| LAYER 4: UPDATE IN-WORK LEG                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateInWorkLeg(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0)
   {
      g_inwork_active = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Last confirmed pivot is our leg start
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[g_pivot_count - 1].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[g_pivot_count - 1].price;
   
   // Initialize if needed
   if(!g_inwork_active || g_inwork_start_bar != leg_start_bar)
   {
      g_inwork_active = true;
      g_inwork_start_bar = leg_start_bar;
      g_inwork_start_price = leg_start_price;
      g_inwork_extreme_high = leg_start_price;
      g_inwork_extreme_low = leg_start_price;
      g_inwork_direction = 0;
   }
   
   // Update extremes using high/low of current bar
   if(high[bar] > g_inwork_extreme_high)
      g_inwork_extreme_high = high[bar];
   
   if(low[bar] < g_inwork_extreme_low)
      g_inwork_extreme_low = low[bar];
   
   // Determine direction: which move is larger from start?
   double up_move = g_inwork_extreme_high - g_inwork_start_price;
   double down_move = g_inwork_start_price - g_inwork_extreme_low;
   
   if(up_move >= down_move)
      g_inwork_direction = 1;  // Up
   else
      g_inwork_direction = -1;  // Down
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| LAYER 5: CALCULATE INDICATOR VALUE                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Set defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_leg_count < Min_ZZ_Legs) return;
   
   // Update in-work leg for this bar
   UpdateInWorkLeg(bar, high, low, rates_total);
   
   if(!g_inwork_active) return;
   
   // Calculate current leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double extreme = (g_inwork_direction > 0) ? g_inwork_extreme_high : g_inwork_extreme_low;
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - g_inwork_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target percentile value
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence percentage
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (g_inwork_direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = g_inwork_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = g_inwork_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| MOCK TRADES                                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_pivot_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[])
{
   // Trade at entry pivot, exit at next pivot
   if(entry_pivot_idx < 0 || entry_pivot_idx + 1 >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   // Don't place same trade twice
   if(entry_pivot_idx <= g_last_trade_pivot) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_pivot_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[entry_pivot_idx + 1].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_pivot_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[entry_pivot_idx + 1].price;
   
   // Date filter (only during initial build)
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0 && time[entry_bar] < Trade_Start_Date)
   {
      g_last_trade_pivot = entry_pivot_idx;
      return;
   }
   
   // Determine direction (compare to previous pivot)
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_pivot_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_pivot_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);  // Trough = buy
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative P/L
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create visual markers
   CreateTradeMarkers(entry_bar, is_buy, pl_pips, rates_total, time);
   
   g_last_trade_pivot = entry_pivot_idx;
   
   Print("Trade placed: entry_bar=", entry_bar, " is_buy=", is_buy, " pl=", DoubleToString(pl_pips, 1), " pips");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateTradeMarkers(int entry_bar, bool is_buy, double pl_pips, int rates_total, const datetime &time[])
{
   // Arrow placed on bar after entry (trade executes after bar closes)
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   double high[], low[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(high, false);
   ArraySetAsSeries(low, false);
   
   if(CopyHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, arrow_bar, 1, high) <= 0) return;
   if(CopyLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, arrow_bar, 1, low) <= 0) return;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * pip_size;
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * pip_size;
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   // Check if already exists
   if(ObjectFind(0, arrow_name) >= 0) return;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[0] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[0] - label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, time[arrow_bar], arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[0] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[0] + label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, time[arrow_bar], arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   // Create P/L label
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, time[arrow_bar], label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   // Alert if enabled
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(time[arrow_bar], TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| MAIN CALCULATION LOOP                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < CONFIRM_DELAY + Min_ZZ_Legs)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total, " (need ", CONFIRM_DELAY + Min_ZZ_Legs, ")");
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(prev_calculated == 0)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      Print("Rates total: ", rates_total);
      
      // Step 1: Freeze all historical bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total - 1; i++)
         FreezeBarAtClose(i, rates_total);
      
      Print("Frozen bars: ", g_last_frozen_bar + 1);
      
      // Step 2: Confirm pivots from frozen data
      for(int i = CONFIRM_DELAY; i < rates_total - 1; i++)
         CheckForNewConfirmedPivots(i, rates_total, time);
      
      Print("Confirmed pivots: ", g_pivot_count);
      Print("Distribution legs: ", g_leg_count);
      Print("Distribution ready: ", g_distribution_ready);
      
      // Step 3: Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
      
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   if(is_new_bar && rates_total >= 2)
   {
      int closed_bar = rates_total - 2;
      
      // Freeze the just-closed bar
      FreezeBarAtClose(closed_bar, rates_total);
      
      // Check for new pivot confirmations
      CheckForNewConfirmedPivots(closed_bar, rates_total, time);
      
      // Recalculate closed bar with new data
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(closed_bar, high, low, rates_total);
   }
   
   // Always update current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Clean Architecture Summary

5 Layers (strict dependencies):

  1. Freeze Layer - Captures ZigZag at bar close, never changes
  2. Pivot Layer - Confirms pivots from frozen data with proper delay
  3. Distribution Layer - Builds from confirmed pivots only
  4. In-Work Layer - Tracks tentative leg using live data + H/L
  5. Indicator Layer - Calculates output from in-work vs distribution

Key differences from old code:

  • No UpdatePivots() - ZigZag never re-read for historical bars
  • Proper CONFIRM_DELAY = ZZ_Depth + ZZ_Backstep
  • State persists correctly across ticks
  • Clear separation of frozen vs live data
  • No historical recalculation after initial build

Ready for testing.

OK, explain how you "confirm pivots" and handle extending the ZZ line ahead before the ZZ indicator actually paints it.
Claude2026-01-09 18:17 UTC

Pivot Confirmation Mechanism

When a bar closes:

void CheckForNewConfirmedPivots(int closed_bar, int rates_total, const datetime &time[])
{
   int candidate_bar = closed_bar - CONFIRM_DELAY;  // Look back 7 bars (ZZ_Depth=5 + ZZ_Backstep=2)
   
   // Check frozen data at that candidate bar
   double pivot_value = g_frozen_zz[candidate_bar];
   if(pivot_value == 0.0) return;  // No pivot there
   
   // Add to confirmed pivots list
   g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = candidate_bar;
   g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = pivot_value;
   g_pivot_count++;
}

Logic:

  • Bar closes at position N
  • Look at frozen ZigZag value at bar N-7 (assuming default params)
  • If that bar has a non-zero value in frozen data, it's a pivot
  • 7-bar buffer ensures ZigZag won't repaint it (ZZ can repaint up to Depth+Backstep bars back)
  • Pivot gets added to confirmed list and never changes

Example timeline:

  • Bar 100 closes → check frozen data at bar 93
  • If g_frozen_zz[93] = 1.2345, confirm it as pivot
  • Add leg from pivot 92 to pivot 93 into distribution
  • Distribution now permanent, won't change

In-Work Leg (Extending Ahead of ZZ Paint)

Problem: Last confirmed pivot might be 10+ bars ago. ZigZag shows a line extending forward, but that line repaints. How do we track the current leg without repainting?

Solution: Ignore live ZigZag completely. Build synthetic leg using high/low tracking.

void UpdateInWorkLeg(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Use last CONFIRMED pivot as leg start (old, frozen, won't change)
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[g_pivot_count - 1].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[g_pivot_count - 1].price;
   
   // Track extremes from that pivot to current bar
   if(high[bar] > g_inwork_extreme_high)
      g_inwork_extreme_high = high[bar];
   
   if(low[bar] < g_inwork_extreme_low)
      g_inwork_extreme_low = low[bar];
   
   // Direction = which extreme is further from start
   double up_move = g_inwork_extreme_high - leg_start_price;
   double down_move = leg_start_price - g_inwork_extreme_low;
   
   if(up_move >= down_move)
      g_inwork_direction = 1;  // Up leg, use high extreme
   else
      g_inwork_direction = -1;  // Down leg, use low extreme
}

Key insight: Never read live ZigZag for current leg calculation. Instead:

  1. Anchor: Last confirmed pivot (frozen 7+ bars ago)
  2. Track: Running highest high and lowest low from anchor to current bar
  3. Direction: Whichever extreme (high or low) is further from anchor
  4. Size: Distance from anchor to current extreme

Example:

  • Last confirmed pivot: bar 93, price 1.2345
  • Current bar: 105
  • Highs since bar 93: max = 1.2389
  • Lows since bar 93: min = 1.2310
  • Up move: 1.2389 - 1.2345 = 0.0044 (44 pips)
  • Down move: 1.2345 - 1.2310 = 0.0035 (35 pips)
  • Direction: UP (44 > 35)
  • Current leg size: 44 pips
  • Compare to distribution percentile → output confidence value

This synthetic leg:

  • Updates every tick (smooth, responsive)
  • Never repaints (anchored to frozen pivot)
  • Doesn't depend on ZigZag painting ahead
  • Resets only when new pivot confirmed (7+ bars later)

Contrast with broken approach:

  • Old code tried to read live ZigZag endpoint
  • That endpoint changes as ZigZag repaints
  • Historical bars see different endpoints on replay
  • Result: repainting

My approach:

  • Completely ignore live ZigZag for in-work leg
  • Use only confirmed pivot + manual high/low tracking
  • Result: no repainting, smooth updates

Understood. Arrow and label both placed at the same bar (arrow_bar = pivot_bar + 1), just different price offsets. Here's the corrected version with original arrow/label logic restored and diagnostic logging added:

Millard2026-01-09 18:34 UTC
I don't want the code yet. I have more questions. Explain in simple English why the indicator line was pegged at 100.
Claude2026-01-09 18:35 UTC

Looking at the code, the in-work leg tracking is accumulating extremes incorrectly.

What should happen:

  • Last confirmed pivot at bar 90, price 1.2000
  • Current bar 100
  • Calculate: max high from bar 90-100, min low from bar 90-100
  • Leg size = distance from 1.2000 to whichever extreme is larger

What my code actually does:

  • Initialize extremes when first pivot confirmed (say bar 10)
  • Bar 11: update extremes with bar 11's high/low
  • Bar 12: update extremes with bar 12's high/low (keeps accumulating)
  • Bar 13: update extremes with bar 13's high/low (still accumulating)
  • ...
  • Bar 100: extremes now contain the max/min of bars 10-100, not just bars 90-100

Result:

  • Leg size calculated from bar 10 to bar 100 (entire chart history)
  • This distance is massive (90 bars of movement)
  • Divided by target percentile value (maybe 50 pips for a typical leg)
  • Ratio = 5000 pips / 50 pips = 10,000%
  • MathMin(100.0, 10000) = 100
  • Every bar shows 100

Fix needed: For each bar calculation, recalculate extremes fresh by scanning from leg_start_bar to current bar, not accumulating across all bars.

Millard2026-01-09 18:37 UTC
* Calculate: max high from bar 90-100, min low from bar 90-100 * Leg size = distance from 1.2000 to whichever extreme is larger The calculation flips between highs and lows depending on the direction of the final confirmed leg. I don't see that in what you said.
Claude2026-01-09 18:37 UTC

You're absolutely right. I was sloppy.

Correct logic:

  1. Determine last pivot type by comparing to previous pivot:

    • If pivot[n] > pivot[n-1] → last pivot is a HIGH (peak)
    • If pivot[n] < pivot[n-1] → last pivot is a LOW (trough)

  2. Track appropriate extreme:

    • If last pivot is HIGH → current leg goes DOWN → track lowest low from pivot to current bar
    • If last pivot is LOW → current leg goes UP → track highest high from pivot to current bar

  3. Calculate leg size:

    • Down leg: leg_size = pivot_price - lowest_low
    • Up leg: leg_size = highest_high - pivot_price

Not "whichever is larger" - it's the extreme in the direction the leg is traveling based on confirmed pivot pattern.

My code was trying to track both extremes and pick the larger move, which is wrong. It should pick the extreme based on the established direction from confirmed pivots.

Millard2026-01-09 18:37 UTC
You said this applies to in-work leg tracking, right?
Claude2026-01-09 18:37 UTC

Yes, correct. This applies to in-work leg tracking only.

Confirmed legs (already in distribution):

  • Both endpoints are confirmed pivots from frozen data
  • Direction already known from the two pivot prices
  • Leg size = absolute difference between the two pivots
  • Never changes, already calculated correctly

In-work leg (extends from last confirmed pivot to current bar):

  • Start point: last confirmed pivot (frozen, known)
  • End point: current bar (moving, live)
  • Direction: determined by comparing last confirmed pivot to previous confirmed pivot
  • Track: the appropriate extreme (high or low) based on that direction
  • This is what's broken in my code - it's not respecting the confirmed direction

The in-work leg needs to "continue" in the direction established by the last confirmed leg pattern.

What I meant:

  • pivot[count-1] = last confirmed pivot (where in-work leg starts)
  • pivot[count-2] = previous confirmed pivot (tells us direction)

Compare these two confirmed pivots:

  • If pivot[count-1] > pivot[count-2] → last confirmed pivot is a HIGH → in-work leg goes DOWN
  • If pivot[count-1] < pivot[count-2] → last confirmed pivot is a LOW → in-work leg goes UP

Then track the appropriate extreme from pivot[count-1].bar to current bar based on that direction.

There is no pivot being confirmed in the in-work zone - we're using the relationship between the last two already confirmed pivots to determine which direction to track.

Millard2026-01-09 18:43 UTC

Each entry in pivot[] is a confirmed ZigZag turning point.

pivot[i].bar = bar index where the pivot occurred pivot[i].price = price at that pivot

These are pivots from frozen ZigZag data that passed the CONFIRM_DELAY (7 bars) without repainting.

pivot[0] = oldest confirmed pivot pivot[count-1] = most recent confirmed pivot

Each represents a point where ZigZag changed direction (peak or trough).

Millard2026-01-09 18:52 UTC

Claude2026-01-09 18:52 UTC

Start at the last confirmed pivot (7+ bars ago, won't repaint).

Look at the pivot before that to determine direction:

  • If last pivot higher than previous → last pivot is a peak → current leg goes down
  • If last pivot lower than previous → last pivot is a trough → current leg goes up

Scan every bar from that last confirmed pivot to the current bar:

  • Down leg: find the lowest low in that range
  • Up leg: find the highest high in that range

Leg size = distance from confirmed pivot price to that extreme.

Compare leg size to the distribution percentile to get confidence percentage.

Updates every tick as price moves, but always anchored to the same confirmed pivot until next pivot confirms 7+ bars later.

Millard2026-01-09 18:58 UTC
You've got confirmed pivots in pivot[], right? So what does 7+ bars have to do with it?  I think the problem here is that there are three cases: 1. The ZZ indicator registers a pivot that never changes. 2. It registers a pivot but several bars pass with no subsequent ZZ leg (bars on the chart without a ZZ leg portion). 3. It registers a pivot and starts a ZZ leg that changes as bars develop and subsequent bars bet painted. 4. It registers a pivot, (cases 2 or 3) but then price heads further in the direction of the previous leg, so it extends the previous leg to the new extreme, erasing the tentative leg it might have started painting, which means erasing the old "confirmed" pivot point in the process. We need to handle all those cases. Your code doesn't, yet.
Claude2026-01-09 18:58 UTC

You're absolutely right. I was oversimplifying.

The 7-bar delay is necessary but not sufficient.

Real confirmation requirements:

  1. Pivot appeared 7+ bars ago (passed repainting window for that specific bar)
  2. ZigZag has thrown a subsequent endpoint in the opposite direction (proves the leg completed)
  3. That subsequent endpoint is also far enough back to be stable

Without requirement #2: A "pivot" might just be ZigZag starting to turn, but then price reverses and ZigZag extends the previous leg, erasing that tentative pivot entirely (your case 4).

Current flaw in my code: I check if frozen data has a non-zero value 7 bars back and call it "confirmed." But I never verify that ZigZag actually completed a leg from that pivot. It might have been erased.

What I should do: Only confirm a pivot when:

  • It's 7+ bars old (frozen)
  • ZigZag has painted a new pivot in the opposite direction
  • That new pivot is also stable (7+ bars old)

So I'm always confirming pivots one leg behind - only confirm pivot N when pivot N+1 becomes stable.

Is that the correct understanding?

Millard2026-01-09 19:04 UTC
I don't think your "7-bar delay" has anything to do with it. 1. Track the ZZ pivots 2. The only pivot that could possibly get redone is the last. 3. If the last ZZ pivot given by the iCustom invocation of the ZZ indicator doesn't match the last one you tracked, you know it was redone. 4. There's no need for a "delay" of any sort. 5. If the ZZ indicator changes the ZZ line, erasing a pivot, the distributions need to be adjusted accurately as soon as the newly lengthened leg is itself confirmed, so remove effects of previous "confirmed" pivot and update with new confirmation. This can happen an indeterminate number of times. 6. The in-work leg is internal, never mixed with confirmed legs, an attempt to minimize HC indicator lag. Is this making sense?
Claude2026-01-09 19:04 UTC

Yes, perfectly clear now.

Real approach:

  1. Read ZigZag buffer each calculation
  2. Extract all current pivots
  3. Compare last pivot to previously tracked last pivot
  4. If different (bar or price changed) → previous last pivot was erased, remove it and its leg from distribution
  5. Confirm all pivots except the last one (second-to-last and earlier are stable)
  6. Build distribution from confirmed pivots only
  7. In-work leg: from last confirmed pivot (second-to-last in current ZigZag) to current bar using high/low
  8. Never mix in-work into distribution

No fixed delay, just watch for ZigZag changes and react immediately.

Millard2026-01-09 19:07 UTC

Claude2026-01-09 19:07 UTC

Yes, I was conflating initial build with live mode.

Correct flow:

Initial build (historical):

  • Read entire ZigZag buffer
  • Extract all pivots, store in pivot[]
  • Build distribution from all confirmed pivots (all except last)
  • One-time process

Live mode:

  • Only track from last ZigZag pivot forward to current bar
  • Watch for last pivot changing (compare to previously stored)
  • If changed: adjust distribution (remove old leg, add new if confirmed)
  • In-work leg: last confirmed pivot to current bar

I was trying to do live-mode tracking during the initial build, which is wrong.

   SetIndexBuffer(0, Historic_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX);

   SetIndexBuffer(9, CumulativeGain, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);

   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);

   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_COLOR_INDEXES, 2);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)

   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM v8.5");

   g_last_tracked_pivot_bar = -1;
   g_last_tracked_pivot_price = 0.0;

   g_initial_build_done = false;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   Print("HoldConfidence v8.5 initialized");
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Extract all pivots from ZigZag buffer                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   // Extract all non-zero values
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
   
   Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots from ZigZag");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from confirmed pivots (all except last)       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Add legs from all confirmed pivots (all except last)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i+1].price - g_pivots[i].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(g_leg_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      if(confirmed_count > 0)
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[confirmed_count - 1].bar;
      
      Print("Distribution ready: ", g_leg_count, " legs, start bar: ", g_indicator_start_bar);
   }
   else
   {
      Print("Distribution building: ", g_leg_count, "/", Min_ZZ_Legs, " legs");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTradesFromConfirmedPivots(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;  // All except last
   
   // Place trades for any new confirmed pivots
   for(int i = g_last_trade_pivot_idx + 1; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   // Date filter
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0 && time[entry_bar] < Trade_Start_Date)
      return;
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create markers (using original working code)
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * pip_size;
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * pip_size;
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   if(ObjectFind(0, arrow_name) >= 0) return;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate in-work leg for current bar                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // Last confirmed pivot is second-to-last in pivot array
   int last_confirmed_idx = g_pivot_count - 2;
   if(last_confirmed_idx < 0) return;
   
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_confirmed_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_confirmed_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction from previous pivot
   int direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
   if(last_confirmed_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[last_confirmed_idx - 1].price;
      if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
         direction = -1;  // Last pivot is peak, leg goes down
      else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
         direction = 1;   // Last pivot is trough, leg goes up
   }
   
   if(direction == 0) return;
   
   // Find appropriate extreme from leg start to current bar
   double extreme = leg_start_price;
   
   if(direction > 0)
   {
      // Up leg - find highest high
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(high[i] > extreme)
            extreme = high[i];
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Down leg - find lowest low
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(low[i] < extreme)
            extreme = low[i];
      }
   }
   
   // Calculate leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target from distribution
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if last pivot changed (live mode)                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_last_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_last_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   // First time tracking
   if(g_last_tracked_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_tracked_pivot_bar = current_last_bar;
      g_last_tracked_pivot_price = current_last_price;
      return;
   }
   
   // Check if changed
   if(current_last_bar != g_last_tracked_pivot_bar || 
      MathAbs(current_last_price - g_last_tracked_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_tracked_pivot_bar, 
            " price ", DoubleToString(g_last_tracked_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_last_bar,
            " price ", DoubleToString(current_last_price, _Digits));
      
      // Rebuild distribution (removes old last leg, adds new if confirmed)
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Rebuild mock trades
      g_cum_pl = 0.0;
      g_cum_gain = 0.0;
      g_cum_loss = 0.0;
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
      ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
      ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
      PlaceMockTradesFromConfirmedPivots(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Update tracking
      g_last_tracked_pivot_bar = current_last_bar;
      g_last_tracked_pivot_price = current_last_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < Min_ZZ_Legs + 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      // Extract all pivots from ZigZag
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      
      // Build distribution from confirmed pivots
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Place mock trades
      PlaceMockTradesFromConfirmedPivots(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_tracked_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_tracked_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      Print("Pivots: ", g_pivot_count, " Legs: ", g_leg_count);
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      // Re-extract pivots to check for changes
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      
      // Check if last pivot changed
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

This version:

  1. Initial build: Extracts all pivots, builds distribution from all except last
  2. Live mode: Re-extracts pivots each new bar, checks if last pivot changed, rebuilds distribution if needed
  3. In-work leg: Uses last confirmed pivot (second-to-last), determines direction from previous pivot, scans high/low appropriately
  4. Arrow placement: Original working code restored - same bar, different price offsets
Millard2026-01-09 19:14 UTC
In-work leg: Uses last confirmed pivot (second-to-last), determines direction from previous pivot, scans high/low appropriately that reads as self-contradictory, restate it please
Claude2026-01-09 19:14 UTC

In-work leg: Starts at second-to-last pivot in array (last confirmed pivot), determines direction by comparing it to third-to-last pivot, scans appropriate extreme (high for up-leg, low for down-leg) from that start pivot to current bar.

Millard2026-01-09 19:20 UTC
that's an unnecessary lag. in-work is based on the last confirmed pivot, not second-last.  two options: A. make pivot[] strictly frozen pivots, meaning that logic needs to handle the last pivot painted by the ZZ indicator outside of using the pivot[] array. This makes the pivot[] array not only holding confirmed pivots, but pivots that cannot ever change. In other words, frozen. B. Add the last painted pivot point to the pivot[] array, keeping in mind it might get updated. Until the ZZ indicator repaints a leg, it will remain untouched. If ZZ paints a new pivot, the last entry in pivot[] needs to be changed, triggering a recalc that will affect nothing prior to the START of the now-modified ZZ leg. I lean towards B, but maybe A is more reliable?  What do you think?
Claude2026-01-09 19:20 UTC

Option B is better.

Reasoning:

A (strict frozen):

  • Requires separate tracking for "current tentative pivot"
  • More state variables
  • Duplicate logic (pivot[] handling + tentative pivot handling)
  • Still need to detect ZZ changes anyway

B (mutable last entry):

  • Single source of truth: pivot[] = current ZZ state
  • In-work leg uses pivot[count-1] directly (most responsive)
  • Distribution uses pivot[0] through pivot[count-2] (remains stable)
  • When pivot[count-1] changes: rebuild distribution, recalc from that point forward
  • Simpler state model

Key insight: We're already re-extracting all pivots from ZZ each bar. Embracing that pivot[count-1] is tentative is simpler than maintaining parallel tracking.

Implementation:

  • pivot[] = all ZZ pivots (last one mutable)
  • Distribution = legs between pivot[0...count-2]
  • In-work = pivot[count-1] to current bar
  • On change: last entry gets new bar/price, triggers rebuild

Recommend B.

Millard2026-01-09 19:21 UTC
OK, make it so
Claude2026-01-09 19:23 UTC

#property indicator_type10  DRAW_NONE

input group "ZigZag Parameters"
input int    ZZ_Depth      = 5;
input int    ZZ_Deviation  = 4;
input int    ZZ_Backstep   = 2;

input group "Analysis Parameters"
input int      Min_ZZ_Legs      = 300;
input datetime Trade_Start_Date = 0;
input double   ConfidenceTargetPercentile = 60.0;

input group "Mock Trades"
input bool   Enter_Mock_Trades = true;

input group "Alerts"
input bool   Alert_On_Trade_Arrow = true;
input bool   Alert_Only_Realtime  = true;

input group "Mock Trade Markers"
input double TradeMarkerOffsetPips = 1.5;
input double TradeLabelOffsetPips  = 3.0;

input group "CSV Export"
input bool   Export_CSV    = false;
input string CSV_Filename  = "HoldConfidence_Data.csv";

input group "Pivot soon (historic) Signal Settings"
input bool   Historic_Show    = false;
input color  Historic_Color   = clrDodgerBlue;
input ENUM_LINE_STYLE Historic_Style = STYLE_SOLID;
int    Historic_Width   = 2;

input group "Reversal Probability Line Settings"
input color  Combined_Up_Color   = clrLime;
input color  Combined_Down_Color = clrRed;
input ENUM_LINE_STYLE Combined_Style = STYLE_SOLID;
input int    Combined_Width = 3;

//--- Indicator buffers
double Historic_Buffer[];
double Combined_Buffer[];
double Combined_Color_Buffer[];
double Debug_PivotIdx[];
double Debug_LegSize[];
double Debug_LegStart[];
double Debug_LegDistCount[];
double Debug_PivotCount[];
double CumulativePL[];
double CumulativeGain[];
double CumulativeLoss[];

//--- ZigZag handle
int zz_handle;

//--- Pivot tracking (ALL pivots including last tentative one)
struct Pivot
{
   int bar;
   double price;
};
Pivot g_pivots[];
int g_pivot_count = 0;

//--- Track last pivot for change detection
int g_last_pivot_bar = -1;
double g_last_pivot_price = 0.0;

//--- Distribution (from confirmed pivots only: 0 through count-2)
double g_leg_sizes[];
int g_leg_count = 0;
bool g_distribution_ready = false;
int g_indicator_start_bar = 0;

//--- Mock trade tracking
double g_cum_pl = 0.0;
double g_cum_gain = 0.0;
double g_cum_loss = 0.0;
int g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;

//--- State tracking
bool g_alerts_armed = false;
datetime g_last_bar_time = 0;
bool g_csv_exported = false;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   SetIndexBuffer(0, Historic_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX);
   SetIndexBuffer(3, Debug_PivotIdx, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(4, Debug_LegSize, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(5, Debug_LegStart, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(6, Debug_LegDistCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(7, Debug_PivotCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(8, CumulativePL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(9, CumulativeGain, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   
   PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotIdx");
   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize");
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "LegStart");
   PlotIndexSetString(5, PLOT_LABEL, "DistCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(6, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativePL");
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeGain");
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeLoss (neg)");
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, Historic_Show ? DRAW_LINE : DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Historic_Width);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_COLOR_INDEXES, 2);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM v8.6");
   
   // Initialize state
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   g_last_pivot_bar = -1;
   g_last_pivot_price = 0.0;
   g_leg_count = 0;
   g_distribution_ready = false;
   g_indicator_start_bar = 0;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_bar_time = 0;
   g_csv_exported = false;
   g_initial_build_done = false;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   Print("HoldConfidence v8.6 initialized");
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Extract ALL pivots from ZigZag buffer (including last tentative)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   // Extract all non-zero values (including last tentative pivot)
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from CONFIRMED pivots only (0 through count-2)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Confirmed pivots: all except last (0 through count-2)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Build legs between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i+1].price - g_pivots[i].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(g_leg_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      if(confirmed_count > 0)
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[confirmed_count - 1].bar;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   // Reset cumulative
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   // Clear existing arrows/labels
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   // Confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Place trades between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   // Date filter
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0 && time[entry_bar] < Trade_Start_Date)
      return;
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);  // Trough = buy
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create markers
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate in-work leg from LAST pivot (tentative, mutable)       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // In-work leg starts at LAST pivot (count-1, the tentative one)
   int last_pivot_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction from previous pivot (count-2)
   int direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
   if(last_pivot_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx - 1].price;
      if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
         direction = -1;  // Last pivot is peak, leg goes down
      else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
         direction = 1;   // Last pivot is trough, leg goes up
   }
   
   if(direction == 0) return;
   
   // Find appropriate extreme from leg start to current bar
   double extreme = leg_start_price;
   
   if(direction > 0)
   {
      // Up leg - find highest high
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(high[i] > extreme)
            extreme = high[i];
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Down leg - find lowest low
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(low[i] < extreme)
            extreme = low[i];
      }
   }
   
   // Calculate leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target from distribution
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Recalculate bars from leg start forward                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RecalculateFromLegStart(int leg_start_bar, int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   for(int i = leg_start_bar; i < rates_total; i++)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if last pivot changed                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   // First time tracking
   if(g_last_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
      return;
   }
   
   // Check if changed
   if(current_bar != g_last_pivot_bar || MathAbs(current_price - g_last_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_pivot_bar, 
            " @ ", DoubleToString(g_last_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_bar,
            " @ ", DoubleToString(current_price, _Digits));
      
      // Rebuild distribution (excludes new last pivot)
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Rebuild mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Recalculate indicator from OLD leg start forward
      int recalc_from = MathMin(g_last_pivot_bar, current_bar);
      RecalculateFromLegStart(recalc_from, rates_total, high, low);
      
      // Update tracking
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < Min_ZZ_Legs + 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      // Extract all pivots (including last tentative)
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      // Build distribution (from confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2)
      BuildDistribution();
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready);
      
      // Place mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   
   // Re-extract pivots every bar to catch changes
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Key changes:

  1. pivot[] = ALL ZZ pivots including last (tentative/mutable)
  2. Distribution built from pivot[0] through pivot[count-2] only
  3. In-work leg uses pivot[count-1] (last, most responsive)
  4. On change: Rebuild distribution, rebuild trades, recalc from old leg start forward
  5. No artificial lag - in-work uses latest ZZ pivot immediately

   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM v8.6");

   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");

}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
