//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Extract ALL pivots from ZigZag buffer (including last tentative)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   // Extract all non-zero values (including last tentative pivot)
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from CONFIRMED pivots only (0 through count-2)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Confirmed pivots: all except last (0 through count-2)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Build legs between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i+1].price - g_pivots[i].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(g_leg_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      if(confirmed_count > 0)
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[confirmed_count - 1].bar;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   // Reset cumulative
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   // Clear existing arrows/labels
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   // Confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Place trades between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   // Date filter
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0 && time[entry_bar] < Trade_Start_Date)
      return;
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);  // Trough = buy
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create markers
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset - label_offset;  // Further below arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset + label_offset;  // Further above arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }   
   
//   if(is_buy)
//   {
//      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
//      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - label_offset;
//      
//      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
//      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
//      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
//      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
//   }
//   else
//   {
//      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
//      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + label_offset;
//      
//      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
//      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
//      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
//      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
//   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate in-work leg from LAST pivot (tentative, mutable)       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // In-work leg starts at LAST pivot (count-1, the tentative one)
   int last_pivot_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction from previous pivot (count-2)
   int direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
   if(last_pivot_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx - 1].price;
      if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
         direction = -1;  // Last pivot is peak, leg goes down
      else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
         direction = 1;   // Last pivot is trough, leg goes up
   }
   
   if(direction == 0) return;
   
   // Find appropriate extreme from leg start to current bar
   double extreme = leg_start_price;
   
   if(direction > 0)
   {
      // Up leg - find highest high
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(high[i] > extreme)
            extreme = high[i];
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Down leg - find lowest low
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(low[i] < extreme)
            extreme = low[i];
      }
   }
   
   // Calculate leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target from distribution
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Recalculate bars from leg start forward                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RecalculateFromLegStart(int leg_start_bar, int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   for(int i = leg_start_bar; i < rates_total; i++)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if last pivot changed                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   // First time tracking
   if(g_last_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
      return;
   }
   
   // Check if changed
   if(current_bar != g_last_pivot_bar || MathAbs(current_price - g_last_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_pivot_bar, 
            " @ ", DoubleToString(g_last_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_bar,
            " @ ", DoubleToString(current_price, _Digits));
      
      // Rebuild distribution (excludes new last pivot)
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Rebuild mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Recalculate indicator from OLD leg start forward
      int recalc_from = MathMin(g_last_pivot_bar, current_bar);
      RecalculateFromLegStart(recalc_from, rates_total, high, low);
      
      // Update tracking
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < Min_ZZ_Legs + 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      // Extract all pivots (including last tentative)
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      // Build distribution (from confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2)
      BuildDistribution();
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready);
      
      // Place mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   
   // Re-extract pivots every bar to catch changes
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Version 8.7 - Status meter added:

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                         HoldConfidence MJM.mq5   |
//|                          Hold Confidence Multi-Signal Indicator  |
//|                                         version 8.7              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Hold Confidence Indicator"
#property version   "8.70"

// [Previous property declarations remain same]

// [All previous global variables remain same]

//--- Status meter
string g_status_label_name = "HoldConf_Status";

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateStatusMeter(string message)
{
   if(ObjectFind(0, g_status_label_name) < 0)
   {
      ObjectCreate(0, g_status_label_name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_CORNER, CORNER_LEFT_UPPER);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, 10);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, 25);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrYellow);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 10);
      ObjectSetString(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
   }
   
   ObjectSetString(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, message);
   ChartRedraw(0);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RemoveStatusMeter()
{
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
   ChartRedraw(0);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from CONFIRMED pivots only (0 through count-2)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Confirmed pivots: all except last (0 through count-2)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Build legs between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i+1].price - g_pivots[i].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
      
      // Update status meter during initial build
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (i % 10 == 0 || i == confirmed_count - 2))
      {
         string status = StringFormat("Building distribution... %d/%d legs (%.1f%%)", 
                                     g_leg_count, Min_ZZ_Legs, 
                                     MathMin(100.0, (g_leg_count * 100.0) / Min_ZZ_Legs));
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(g_leg_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      if(confirmed_count > 0)
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[confirmed_count - 1].bar;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   // Reset cumulative
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   // Clear existing arrows/labels
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   // Confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int total_trades = confirmed_count - 1;
   
   // Place trades between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < total_trades; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
      
      // Update status meter during trade placement (only during initial build)
      if(!g_initial_build_done && Enter_Mock_Trades && (i % 5 == 0 || i == total_trades - 1))
      {
         string status = StringFormat("Placing trade arrows... %d/%d (%.1f%%)", 
                                     i + 1, total_trades,
                                     ((i + 1) * 100.0) / total_trades);
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < Min_ZZ_Legs + 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Extracting ZigZag pivots...");
      
      // Extract all pivots (including last tentative)
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      // Build distribution (from confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2)
      BuildDistribution();
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready);
      
      // Place mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      if(Enter_Mock_Trades)
         RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Calculating indicator values...");
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
      {
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
         
         // Update status periodically
         if(i % 100 == 0 || i == rates_total - 1)
         {
            string status = StringFormat("Calculating... %d/%d bars (%.1f%%)",
                                        i + 1, rates_total,
                                        ((i + 1) * 100.0) / rates_total);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
      }
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      // Remove status meter after completion
      RemoveStatusMeter();
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   
   // Re-extract pivots every bar to catch changes
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

Changes in v8.7:

  1. Added UpdateStatusMeter() - Creates/updates yellow label at top-left
  2. Added RemoveStatusMeter() - Removes label when done
  3. Status updates during:
    • Pivot extraction (single message)
    • Distribution building (updates every 10 legs)
    • Trade placement (updates every 5 trades, only if Enter_Mock_Trades enabled)
    • Indicator calculation (updates every 100 bars)
  4. Status meter removed automatically after initial build completes

Version 8.7 - Complete file with status meter:

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                         HoldConfidence MJM.mq5   |
//|                          Hold Confidence Multi-Signal Indicator  |
//|                                         version 8.7              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Hold Confidence Indicator"
#property version   "8.70"
#property indicator_separate_window
#property indicator_buffers 11
#property indicator_plots   10
#property indicator_minimum -15
#property indicator_maximum 115

#property indicator_level1 0.0
#property indicator_level2 100.0
#property indicator_levelcolor clrGray
#property indicator_levelstyle STYLE_DOT

#property indicator_label1  "Pivot soon (historic)"
#property indicator_type1   DRAW_LINE
#property indicator_label2  "Reversal Probability"
#property indicator_type2   DRAW_COLOR_LINE
#property indicator_label3  "PivotIdx"
#property indicator_type3   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label4  "LegSize"  
#property indicator_type4   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label5  "LegStart"
#property indicator_type5   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label6  "DistCount"
#property indicator_type6   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label7  "PivotCount"
#property indicator_type7   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label8  "CumulativePL"
#property indicator_type8   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label9  "CumulativeGain"
#property indicator_type9   DRAW_NONE
#property indicator_label10 "CumulativeLoss (neg)"
#property indicator_type10  DRAW_NONE

input group "ZigZag Parameters"
input int    ZZ_Depth      = 5;
input int    ZZ_Deviation  = 4;
input int    ZZ_Backstep   = 2;

input group "Analysis Parameters"
input int      Min_ZZ_Legs      = 300;
input datetime Trade_Start_Date = 0;
input double   ConfidenceTargetPercentile = 60.0;

input group "Mock Trades"
input bool   Enter_Mock_Trades = true;

input group "Alerts"
input bool   Alert_On_Trade_Arrow = true;
input bool   Alert_Only_Realtime  = true;

input group "Mock Trade Markers"
input double TradeMarkerOffsetPips = 10.0;
input double TradeLabelOffsetPips  = 5.0;

input group "CSV Export"
input bool   Export_CSV    = false;
input string CSV_Filename  = "HoldConfidence_Data.csv";

input group "Pivot soon (historic) Signal Settings"
input bool   Historic_Show    = false;
input color  Historic_Color   = clrDodgerBlue;
input ENUM_LINE_STYLE Historic_Style = STYLE_SOLID;
input int    Historic_Width   = 2;

input group "Reversal Probability Line Settings"
input color  Combined_Up_Color   = clrLime;
input color  Combined_Down_Color = clrRed;
input ENUM_LINE_STYLE Combined_Style = STYLE_SOLID;
input int    Combined_Width = 3;

//--- Indicator buffers
double Historic_Buffer[];
double Combined_Buffer[];
double Combined_Color_Buffer[];
double Debug_PivotIdx[];
double Debug_LegSize[];
double Debug_LegStart[];
double Debug_LegDistCount[];
double Debug_PivotCount[];
double CumulativePL[];
double CumulativeGain[];
double CumulativeLoss[];

//--- ZigZag handle
int zz_handle;

//--- Pivot tracking (ALL pivots including last tentative one)
struct Pivot
{
   int bar;
   double price;
};
Pivot g_pivots[];
int g_pivot_count = 0;

//--- Track last pivot for change detection
int g_last_pivot_bar = -1;
double g_last_pivot_price = 0.0;

//--- Distribution (from confirmed pivots only: 0 through count-2)
double g_leg_sizes[];
int g_leg_count = 0;
bool g_distribution_ready = false;
int g_indicator_start_bar = 0;

//--- Mock trade tracking
double g_cum_pl = 0.0;
double g_cum_gain = 0.0;
double g_cum_loss = 0.0;
int g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;

//--- State tracking
bool g_alerts_armed = false;
datetime g_last_bar_time = 0;
bool g_csv_exported = false;
bool g_initial_build_done = false;

//--- Status meter
string g_status_label_name = "HoldConf_Status";

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateStatusMeter(string message)
{
   if(ObjectFind(0, g_status_label_name) < 0)
   {
      ObjectCreate(0, g_status_label_name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_CORNER, CORNER_LEFT_UPPER);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, 10);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, 25);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrYellow);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 10);
      ObjectSetString(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
   }
   
   ObjectSetString(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, message);
   ChartRedraw(0);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RemoveStatusMeter()
{
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
   ChartRedraw(0);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   SetIndexBuffer(0, Historic_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX);
   SetIndexBuffer(3, Debug_PivotIdx, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(4, Debug_LegSize, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(5, Debug_LegStart, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(6, Debug_LegDistCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(7, Debug_PivotCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(8, CumulativePL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(9, CumulativeGain, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   
   PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotIdx");
   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize");
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "LegStart");
   PlotIndexSetString(5, PLOT_LABEL, "DistCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(6, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativePL");
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeGain");
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeLoss (neg)");
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, Historic_Show ? DRAW_LINE : DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Historic_Width);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_COLOR_INDEXES, 2);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM v8.7");
   
   // Initialize state
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   g_last_pivot_bar = -1;
   g_last_pivot_price = 0.0;
   g_leg_count = 0;
   g_distribution_ready = false;
   g_indicator_start_bar = 0;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_bar_time = 0;
   g_csv_exported = false;
   g_initial_build_done = false;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
   
   Print("HoldConfidence v8.7 initialized");
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Extract ALL pivots from ZigZag buffer (including last tentative)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   // Extract all non-zero values (including last tentative pivot)
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from CONFIRMED pivots only (0 through count-2)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Confirmed pivots: all except last (0 through count-2)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Build legs between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i+1].price - g_pivots[i].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
      
      // Update status meter during initial build
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (i % 10 == 0 || i == confirmed_count - 2))
      {
         string status = StringFormat("Building distribution... %d/%d legs (%.1f%%)", 
                                     g_leg_count, Min_ZZ_Legs, 
                                     MathMin(100.0, (g_leg_count * 100.0) / Min_ZZ_Legs));
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(g_leg_count >= Min_ZZ_Legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      if(confirmed_count > 0)
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[confirmed_count - 1].bar;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   // Reset cumulative
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   // Clear existing arrows/labels
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   // Confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int total_trades = confirmed_count - 1;
   
   // Place trades between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < total_trades; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
      
      // Update status meter during trade placement (only during initial build)
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (i % 5 == 0 || i == total_trades - 1))
      {
         string status = StringFormat("Placing trade arrows... %d/%d (%.1f%%)", 
                                     i + 1, total_trades,
                                     ((i + 1) * 100.0) / total_trades);
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   // Date filter
   if(Trade_Start_Date != 0 && time[entry_bar] < Trade_Start_Date)
      return;
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);  // Trough = buy
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create markers
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset - label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset + label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate in-work leg from LAST pivot (tentative, mutable)       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // In-work leg starts at LAST pivot (count-1, the tentative one)
   int last_pivot_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction from previous pivot (count-2)
   int direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
   if(last_pivot_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx - 1].price;
      if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
         direction = -1;  // Last pivot is peak, leg goes down
      else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
         direction = 1;   // Last pivot is trough, leg goes up
   }
   
   if(direction == 0) return;
   
   // Find appropriate extreme from leg start to current bar
   double extreme = leg_start_price;
   
   if(direction > 0)
   {
      // Up leg - find highest high
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(high[i] > extreme)
            extreme = high[i];
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Down leg - find lowest low
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(low[i] < extreme)
            extreme = low[i];
      }
   }
   
   // Calculate leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target from distribution
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Recalculate bars from leg start forward                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RecalculateFromLegStart(int leg_start_bar, int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   for(int i = leg_start_bar; i < rates_total; i++)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if last pivot changed                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   // First time tracking
   if(g_last_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
      return;
   }
   
   // Check if changed
   if(current_bar != g_last_pivot_bar || MathAbs(current_price - g_last_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_pivot_bar, 
            " @ ", DoubleToString(g_last_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_bar,
            " @ ", DoubleToString(current_price, _Digits));
      
      // Rebuild distribution (excludes new last pivot)
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Rebuild mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Recalculate indicator from OLD leg start forward
      int recalc_from = MathMin(g_last_pivot_bar, current_bar);
      RecalculateFromLegStart(recalc_from, rates_total, high, low);
      
      // Update tracking
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < Min_ZZ_Legs + 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Extracting ZigZag pivots...");
      
      // Extract all pivots (including last tentative)
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      // Build distribution (from confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2)
      BuildDistribution();
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready);
      
      // Place mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      if(Enter_Mock_Trades)
         RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Calculating indicator values...");
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
      {
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
         
         // Update status periodically
         if(i % 100 == 0 || i == rates_total - 1)
         {
            string status = StringFormat("Calculating... %d/%d bars (%.1f%%)",
                                        i + 1, rates_total,
                                        ((i + 1) * 100.0) / rates_total);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
      }
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      // Remove status meter after completion
      RemoveStatusMeter();
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   
   // Re-extract pivots every bar to catch changes
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}

void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   // ... [earlier code omitted] ...
   
   // Create markers
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset - label_offset;  // Further below arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset + label_offset;  // Further above arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
}

Key lines for label placement:

  • Buy labels: label_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset - label_offset;
  • Sell labels: label_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset + label_offset;
  • Anchor: ANCHOR_RIGHT (places text to the left of the coordinates)

   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM",

   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
   
   Print("HoldConfidence v8.8 initialized - Min_ZZ_Legs: ", Min_ZZ_Legs, " (0=all history)");
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Extract ALL pivots from ZigZag buffer (including last tentative)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   // Extract all non-zero values (including last tentative pivot)
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from CONFIRMED pivots only (0 through count-2)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Confirmed pivots: all except last (0 through count-2)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int total_legs = confirmed_count - 1;
   
   // Build legs between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = 0; i < total_legs; i++)
   {
      // Update status BEFORE adding leg
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (i % 10 == 0 || i == total_legs - 1))
      {
         int target = (Min_ZZ_Legs == 0) ? total_legs : Min_ZZ_Legs;
         string status = StringFormat("Building distribution... %d/%d legs (%.1f%%)", 
                                     i + 1, target, 
                                     MathMin(100.0, ((i + 1) * 100.0) / target));
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
      
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i+1].price - g_pivots[i].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready
   if(g_leg_count >= g_effective_min_legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      if(confirmed_count > 0)
         g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[confirmed_count - 1].bar;
      
      // If Trade_Start_Date = 0, set to bar after min legs reached
      if(g_trade_start_date == 0 && !g_initial_build_done)
      {
         // Find the pivot index where we reached min legs
         int legs_so_far = 0;
         for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
         {
            legs_so_far++;
            if(legs_so_far >= g_effective_min_legs)
            {
               // Start trading from next pivot
               if(i + 1 < confirmed_count)
                  g_trade_start_date = TimeCurrent();  // Will use pivot index check instead
               break;
            }
         }
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   
   // Reset cumulative
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   // Clear existing arrows/labels
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   // Confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int total_trades = confirmed_count - 1;
   
   // Determine starting pivot index for trades
   int start_pivot_idx = 0;
   
   if(Trade_Start_Date == 0)
   {
      // Start after Min_ZZ_Legs reached (or immediately if Min_ZZ_Legs = 0)
      if(Min_ZZ_Legs > 0)
      {
         start_pivot_idx = Min_ZZ_Legs;  // First trade at pivot Min_ZZ_Legs
         if(start_pivot_idx >= total_trades)
            start_pivot_idx = 0;  // Fallback if not enough legs
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Find first pivot at or after Trade_Start_Date
      for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count; i++)
      {
         if(time[g_pivots[i].bar] >= Trade_Start_Date)
         {
            start_pivot_idx = i;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
   
   // Place trades between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = start_pivot_idx; i < total_trades; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
      
      // Update status meter during trade placement (only during initial build)
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (i % 5 == 0 || i == total_trades - 1))
      {
         int trades_placed = i - start_pivot_idx + 1;
         int total_to_place = total_trades - start_pivot_idx;
         string status = StringFormat("Placing trade arrows... %d/%d (%.1f%%)", 
                                     trades_placed, total_to_place,
                                     (trades_placed * 100.0) / total_to_place);
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);  // Trough = buy
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create markers
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset - label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset + label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate in-work leg from LAST pivot (tentative, mutable)       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // In-work leg starts at LAST pivot (count-1, the tentative one)
   int last_pivot_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction from previous pivot (count-2)
   int direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
   if(last_pivot_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx - 1].price;
      if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
         direction = -1;  // Last pivot is peak, leg goes down
      else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
         direction = 1;   // Last pivot is trough, leg goes up
   }
   
   if(direction == 0) return;
   
   // Find appropriate extreme from leg start to current bar
   double extreme = leg_start_price;
   
   if(direction > 0)
   {
      // Up leg - find highest high
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(high[i] > extreme)
            extreme = high[i];
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Down leg - find lowest low
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(low[i] < extreme)
            extreme = low[i];
      }
   }
   
   // Calculate leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target from distribution
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Recalculate bars from leg start forward                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RecalculateFromLegStart(int leg_start_bar, int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   for(int i = leg_start_bar; i < rates_total; i++)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if last pivot changed                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   // First time tracking
   if(g_last_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
      return;
   }
   
   // Check if changed
   if(current_bar != g_last_pivot_bar || MathAbs(current_price - g_last_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_pivot_bar, 
            " @ ", DoubleToString(g_last_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_bar,
            " @ ", DoubleToString(current_price, _Digits));
      
      // Rebuild distribution (excludes new last pivot)
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Rebuild mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Recalculate indicator from OLD leg start forward
      int recalc_from = MathMin(g_last_pivot_bar, current_bar);
      RecalculateFromLegStart(recalc_from, rates_total, high, low);
      
      // Update tracking
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Extracting ZigZag pivots...");
      
      // Extract all pivots (including last tentative)
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      // Build distribution (from confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2)
      BuildDistribution();
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready);
      
      // Place mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      if(Enter_Mock_Trades)
         RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Calculating indicator values...");
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
      {
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
         
         // Update status periodically
         if(i % 100 == 0 || i == rates_total - 1)
         {
            string status = StringFormat("Calculating... %d/%d bars (%.1f%%)",
                                        i + 1, rates_total,
                                        ((i + 1) * 100.0) / rates_total);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
      }
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      // Remove status meter after completion
      RemoveStatusMeter();
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   
   // Re-extract pivots every bar to catch changes
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}

Claude2026-01-09 20:59 UTC

Problems identified:

1. Progress meter

  • Shows 10451/300 (100.0%) because it builds ALL legs from history (10,451) but displays against Min_ZZ_Legs (300)
  • Should show progress ONLY up to Min_ZZ_Legs, then stop updating

2. Min_ZZ_Legs not limiting distribution

  • Current code: Checks if(g_leg_count >= g_effective_min_legs) to mark "ready" but continues building ALL legs
  • Should: STOP building distribution after Min_ZZ_Legs reached. Only use first 300 legs for distribution, ignore remaining 10,000+ legs in history

3. Trade placement calculation

start_pivot_idx = Min_ZZ_Legs;  // First trade at pivot Min_ZZ_Legs

This sets start to pivot index 300, which places first trade at leg 300→301. This is mathematically correct BUT only if distribution was built from first 300 legs (which it wasn't - see problem #2).

4. Arrows placed at bar 2237 With ~10,450 total legs and starting at pivot 300, arrows appear very early in history (after 300/10450 = 2.9% of data). This happens because:

  • Distribution incorrectly built from ALL legs
  • Trade start correctly calculated at pivot 300
  • But pivot 300 is very early in the dataset

Root cause: BuildDistribution() never stops at Min_ZZ_Legs. It processes every confirmed pivot in history regardless of Min_ZZ_Legs value.

Fix needed:

  • BuildDistribution() must STOP after processing Min_ZZ_Legs legs (or all legs if Min_ZZ_Legs=0)
  • Progress meter must show correct denominator and stop at 100%
  • Indicator start bar should be at pivot where Min_ZZ_Legs reached

Version 8.9 with proper fixes?

You're right. I had it backwards.

Correct understanding:

Min_ZZ_Legs = minimum threshold to START indicator/trades, not a cap.

  • Distribution builds from ALL available history continuously
  • When leg count reaches Min_ZZ_Legs → mark "ready" and start indicator output
  • If Min_ZZ_Legs = 0 → ready immediately, process all history from start
  • Indicator only outputs values after the bar where Min_ZZ_Legs threshold reached
  • If Trade_Start_Date = 0 → start trades at the bar where Min_ZZ_Legs reached

Progress meter should:

  • Count up to Min_ZZ_Legs: "Building... 150/300 (50%)"
  • Hit threshold: "Building... 300/300 (100%)"
  • Continue building but stop updating meter OR change message to "Distribution ready: 10451 total legs"

  • Meter shows final count (10,451) against threshold (300) = nonsense percentage
  • Should cap percentage at 100% when threshold reached

Trade placement bug:

  • Starting at pivot index 300 is wrong
  • Should start at the bar where 300th leg was completed, not pivot #300

Millard2026-01-09 21:10 UTC
It's not just the meter. Min_ZZ_Legs says: count back from current until you've got at least Min_ZZ_Legs. The processing is what matters. The meter is just supposed to track the processing. Note that you should go back a reasonably small amount of history to prime the calculations so that by the time you get to the Min_ZZ_Legs leg the internal data won't all be zeroes. That doesn't mean go beyond Min_ZZ_Legs for indicator and arrows. It means go beyond enough so that you can start indicator and arrows accurately by the time you get to Min_ZZ_Legs. Also, the arrow removal logic isn't removing the labels (unless that was a one-time glitch, I had to remove them manually.)
Claude2026-01-09 21:10 UTC

Understood now. Complete misunderstanding on my part.

Correct logic:

Min_ZZ_Legs = 300 means:

  1. Start from CURRENT bar, work BACKWARD through history
  2. Count ZZ legs as you go back
  3. STOP when you've accumulated 300 legs (defines your data window)
  4. Add "priming buffer" (maybe 50 legs?) BEFORE that point so calculations are meaningful by the time you reach leg 300
  5. Build distribution ONLY from those 300 legs (not all 10,451)
  6. Indicator output starts at the bar where 300th leg completed
  7. Trade arrows start at same bar (if Trade_Start_Date = 0)

If Min_ZZ_Legs = 0:

  • Process all available history
  • No backward limit

Progress meter:

  • Tracks accumulation: "Building... 150/300 (50%)"
  • Stops at threshold: "Building... 300/300 (100%)"

Arrow removal bug:

  • Code has both ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_") and ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_")
  • Should work but apparently doesn't
  • Need to verify object name matching

void UpdateStatusMeter(string message)
{
   if(ObjectFind(0, g_status_label_name) < 0)
   {
      ObjectCreate(0, g_status_label_name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_CORNER, CORNER_LEFT_UPPER);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, 10);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, 25);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrYellow);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 10);
      ObjectSetString(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
   }
   
   ObjectSetString(0, g_status_label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, message);
   ChartRedraw(0);
}
