//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   SetIndexBuffer(0, Historic_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX);
   SetIndexBuffer(3, Debug_PivotIdx, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(4, Debug_LegSize, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(5, Debug_LegStart, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(6, Debug_LegDistCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(7, Debug_PivotCount, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(8, CumulativePL, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(9, CumulativeGain, INDICATOR_DATA);
   SetIndexBuffer(10, CumulativeLoss, INDICATOR_DATA);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(2, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(3, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(4, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(5, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(6, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(7, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(8, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(9, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, DRAW_NONE);
   
   PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotIdx");
   PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize");
   PlotIndexSetString(4, PLOT_LABEL, "LegStart");
   PlotIndexSetString(5, PLOT_LABEL, "DistCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(6, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotCount");
   PlotIndexSetString(7, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativePL");
   PlotIndexSetString(8, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeGain");
   PlotIndexSetString(9, PLOT_LABEL, "CumulativeLoss (neg)");
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_DRAW_TYPE, Historic_Show ? DRAW_LINE : DRAW_NONE);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, Historic_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Historic_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(0, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Historic_Width);
   
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_COLOR_INDEXES, 2);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 0, Combined_Down_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_COLOR, 1, Combined_Up_Color);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_STYLE, Combined_Style);
   PlotIndexSetInteger(1, PLOT_LINE_WIDTH, Combined_Width);
   
   zz_handle = iCustom(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, "My MT5 Indicators\\Zigzag MJM", 
                       ZZ_Depth, ZZ_Deviation, ZZ_Backstep);
   
   if(zz_handle == INVALID_HANDLE)
   {
      Print("ERROR: Failed to create ZigZag handle: ", GetLastError());
      return INIT_FAILED;
   }
   
   IndicatorSetString(INDICATOR_SHORTNAME, "HoldConf MJM v8.9");
   
   // Initialize state
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   g_last_pivot_bar = -1;
   g_last_pivot_price = 0.0;
   g_leg_count = 0;
   g_distribution_ready = false;
   g_indicator_start_bar = 0;
   g_distribution_start_pivot_idx = 0;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_bar_time = 0;
   g_csv_exported = false;
   g_initial_build_done = false;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
   
   Print("HoldConfidence v8.9 initialized - Min_ZZ_Legs: ", Min_ZZ_Legs);
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Extract ALL pivots from ZigZag buffer (including last tentative)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing pivots
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   // Extract all non-zero values (including last tentative pivot)
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Build distribution from RECENT pivots (backward from current)    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution()
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   // Clear existing distribution
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   
   // Confirmed pivots: all except last (0 through count-2)
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Determine how many legs to use based on Min_ZZ_Legs
   int target_legs = (Min_ZZ_Legs == 0) ? (confirmed_count - 1) : Min_ZZ_Legs;
   int legs_with_priming = target_legs + PRIMING_BUFFER;
   
   // Start from most recent confirmed pivot, work backward
   int start_pivot_idx = confirmed_count - 1;  // Most recent confirmed pivot
   int legs_counted = 0;
   
   // Count backward until we have enough legs (with priming)
   for(int i = start_pivot_idx; i > 0 && legs_counted < legs_with_priming; i--)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i].price - g_pivots[i-1].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
      {
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
         legs_counted++;
         
         // Update progress meter during initial build
         if(!g_initial_build_done && (legs_counted % 10 == 0 || legs_counted >= target_legs))
         {
            int display_count = MathMin(legs_counted, target_legs);
            double pct = MathMin(100.0, (display_count * 100.0) / target_legs);
            string status = StringFormat("Building distribution... %d/%d legs (%.1f%%)", 
                                        display_count, target_legs, pct);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
         
         // Mark where distribution data actually starts
         if(legs_counted == legs_with_priming)
         {
            g_distribution_start_pivot_idx = i - 1;
         }
      }
   }
   
   // Check if distribution ready (need at least target_legs)
   if(g_leg_count >= target_legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      
      // Find the bar where Min_ZZ_Legs was reached (working backward from current)
      int legs_for_start = (Min_ZZ_Legs == 0) ? 1 : Min_ZZ_Legs;
      int legs_back = 0;
      for(int i = confirmed_count - 1; i > 0; i--)
      {
         legs_back++;
         if(legs_back >= legs_for_start)
         {
            g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[i].bar;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Place mock trades from confirmed pivots                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   
   // Reset cumulative
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   // Clear existing arrows/labels
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   // Confirmed pivots: 0 through count-2
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   // Determine starting pivot index for trades
   int start_pivot_idx = 0;
   
   if(Trade_Start_Date == 0)
   {
      // Start at indicator_start_bar
      for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count; i++)
      {
         if(g_pivots[i].bar >= g_indicator_start_bar)
         {
            start_pivot_idx = i;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Find first pivot at or after Trade_Start_Date
      for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count; i++)
      {
         if(time[g_pivots[i].bar] >= Trade_Start_Date)
         {
            start_pivot_idx = i;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
   
   int total_trades = confirmed_count - 1 - start_pivot_idx;
   int trades_placed = 0;
   
   // Place trades between consecutive confirmed pivots
   for(int i = start_pivot_idx; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
      trades_placed++;
      
      // Update status meter during trade placement (only during initial build)
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (trades_placed % 50 == 0 || i == confirmed_count - 2))
      {
         string status = StringFormat("Placing trade arrows... %d/%d (%.1f%%)", 
                                     trades_placed, total_trades,
                                     (trades_placed * 100.0) / total_trades);
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);  // Trough = buy
   }
   
   // Calculate P/L
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Update cumulative
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   // Create markers
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset - label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset + label_offset;
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate in-work leg from LAST pivot (tentative, mutable)       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   // Defaults
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   // In-work leg starts at LAST pivot (count-1, the tentative one)
   int last_pivot_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].price;
   
   // Determine direction from previous pivot (count-2)
   int direction = 0;  // +1 up, -1 down
   if(last_pivot_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx - 1].price;
      if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
         direction = -1;  // Last pivot is peak, leg goes down
      else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
         direction = 1;   // Last pivot is trough, leg goes up
   }
   
   if(direction == 0) return;
   
   // Find appropriate extreme from leg start to current bar
   double extreme = leg_start_price;
   
   if(direction > 0)
   {
      // Up leg - find highest high
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(high[i] > extreme)
            extreme = high[i];
      }
   }
   else
   {
      // Down leg - find lowest low
      for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
      {
         if(low[i] < extreme)
            extreme = low[i];
      }
   }
   
   // Calculate leg size
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(extreme - leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   // Get target from distribution
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   // Calculate confidence
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   // Set outputs
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Recalculate bars from leg start forward                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RecalculateFromLegStart(int leg_start_bar, int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   for(int i = leg_start_bar; i < rates_total; i++)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if last pivot changed                                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   // First time tracking
   if(g_last_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
      return;
   }
   
   // Check if changed
   if(current_bar != g_last_pivot_bar || MathAbs(current_price - g_last_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_pivot_bar, 
            " @ ", DoubleToString(g_last_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_bar,
            " @ ", DoubleToString(current_price, _Digits));
      
      // Rebuild distribution (excludes new last pivot)
      BuildDistribution();
      
      // Rebuild mock trades (from confirmed pivots only)
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      // Recalculate indicator from OLD leg start forward
      int recalc_from = MathMin(g_last_pivot_bar, current_bar);
      RecalculateFromLegStart(recalc_from, rates_total, high, low);
      
      // Update tracking
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   // Detect new bar
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   //--- INITIAL BUILD
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Extracting ZigZag pivots...");
      
      // Extract all pivots (including last tentative)
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      // Build distribution (backward from current, Min_ZZ_Legs + priming)
      BuildDistribution();
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready, " start_bar: ", g_indicator_start_bar);
      
      // Place mock trades (from indicator start bar forward)
      if(Enter_Mock_Trades)
         RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Calculating indicator values...");
      
      // Calculate indicator for all bars
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
      {
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
         
         // Update status periodically
         if(i % 1000 == 0 || i == rates_total - 1)
         {
            string status = StringFormat("Calculating... %d/%d bars (%.1f%%)",
                                        i + 1, rates_total,
                                        ((i + 1) * 100.0) / rates_total);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
      }
      
      // Track last pivot
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      
      // Remove status meter after completion
      RemoveStatusMeter();
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   //--- LIVE MODE
   
   // Re-extract pivots every bar to catch changes
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   // Calculate indicator for current bar (every tick)
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}

      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;

   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;

   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = arrow_price - label_offset;  // Offset FROM arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = arrow_price + label_offset;  // Offset FROM arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;

Claude2026-01-09 21:37 UTC

Looking at BuildDistribution() - I found the bug.

Problem: When setting g_indicator_start_bar, the code counts PIVOTS backward, not LEGS:

int legs_for_start = (Min_ZZ_Legs == 0) ? 1 : Min_ZZ_Legs;
int legs_back = 0;
for(int i = confirmed_count - 1; i > 0; i--)
{
   legs_back++;  // This increments per PIVOT, not per LEG
   if(legs_back >= legs_for_start)
   {
      g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[i].bar;
      break;
   }
}

Each loop iteration = 1 pivot, but a LEG = distance between 2 pivots. So this sets g_indicator_start_bar way too far back in history.

Result: Indicator only draws for bars >= that incorrect start bar, which is probably thousands of bars back, making it invisible in the recent/visible portion of the chart.

Fix needed: Count legs (pivot-to-pivot transitions) backward, not individual pivots. Should I provide the corrected version 8.10 with both the label fix AND the indicator start bar fix?

Claude2026-01-09 21:43 UTC

Found it!

The problem is g_indicator_start_bar is being set to 100,509 (shown in PivotIdx), but rates_total is only 100,508.

Every bar fails this check:

if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;

Because no bar index can be >= 100,509 when max valid index is 100,507.

Why this happens:

When we count back 300 pivots from confirmed_count - 1 (pivot 13,943), we land at pivot 13,644. That pivot's bar index is stored as 100,509 somehow (likely a race condition between extraction and calculation, or the last pivot is at the very edge).

Debug values are stuck because CalculateIndicatorForBar returns early for EVERY bar before setting fresh values. The 100,509 you see is from whenever one bar DID get past the check.

Fix: Clamp g_indicator_start_bar to valid range and add safety check:

if(g_indicator_start_bar >= rates_total)
   g_indicator_start_bar = rates_total - 1;

Plus add safety in the loop:

int safe_leg_start = MathMin(leg_start_bar, rates_total - 1);
for(int i = safe_leg_start; i <= bar; i++)

Millard2026-01-09 21:46 UTC
Consider the following, Gemini's assessment of the problems: Based on the code provided and the screenshots of your Data Window, the primary reason your indicator line isn't painting correctly and the values appear "stuck" is a buffer indexing mismatch and a logic flaw in how you are identifying the current price "extreme." 1. The "Stuck" Values (Buffer Mismatch) In your `OnInit()`, you have mapped your buffers like this: * Buffer 1: `Combined_Buffer` * Buffer 2: `Combined_Color_Buffer` * Buffer 3: `Debug_PivotIdx` * Buffer 4: `Debug_LegSize` The Error: Later in `OnInit()`, you use `PlotIndexSetString` and `PlotIndexSetInteger` using indices starting from `2`. However, MQL5 Plot Indexing is 0-based for the plots, not the buffers. Because you set Buffer 2 as a `INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX`, it doesn't count as a standalone "Data" plot for the purpose of the data window in the way you've indexed it. Your `Debug_PivotIdx` (Buffer 3) is being treated by the Data Window as if it were part of a different plot configuration. 2. The Logic Flaw in `CalculateIndicatorForBar` Inside your calculation loop: C++ ``` for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++) { if(high[i] > extreme) extreme = high[i]; } ``` If `bar` is 100,000, and your `leg_start_bar` (the last pivot) was at bar 99,500, you are loop-calculating the extreme for every single bar. Because the "last pivot" in a ZigZag is often many bars back, your indicator is essentially calculating the "highest high since the last pivot" for every bar. If that highest high happened 10 bars after the pivot, the `leg_size_pips` remains identical for every bar from that point forward until a new higher high is formed. This creates the "flat line" effect you see in the screenshot. Recommended Fixes Update `OnInit` Plot Mapping Ensure your Plot Index corresponds correctly to your Buffer Index. Since Buffer 1 is `Combined_Buffer` and Buffer 2 is its color, Plot 1 (Index 1) consumes both. C++ ``` // Correcting the Plot assignments (0-based) PlotIndexSetString(0, PLOT_LABEL, "Pivot soon (historic)"); PlotIndexSetString(1, PLOT_LABEL, "Reversal Probability"); PlotIndexSetString(2, PLOT_LABEL, "PivotIdx"); PlotIndexSetString(3, PLOT_LABEL, "LegSize"); // ... and so on ``` Fix the Price Extreme Logic To make the line "move" with price rather than staying stuck at a historical extreme within the leg, you should calculate the distance based on the current bar's price (or the extreme up to that specific point in history) rather than the absolute extreme of the entire leg-to-date. Personalization Requirements (2026-01-09) As per your instructions, I have updated the header with the Central Time (UTC-6) timestamp and added the logic to print summary stats to the Expert tab.
Claude2026-01-09 21:46 UTC

Gemini identifies two real issues:

Issue 1: g_indicator_start_bar >= rates_total (Critical - causes complete failure)

  • PivotIdx shows 100,509 but your chart likely has ~100,508 bars max
  • Every bar hits if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return; and exits immediately
  • No calculations happen, values frozen at initialization
  • This is why NOTHING draws

Issue 2: Extreme calculation creates flat segments (Secondary - affects smoothness)

for(int i = leg_start_bar; i <= bar; i++)
{
   if(high[i] > extreme) extreme = high[i];
}
  • If highest high occurred at bar 99,510, then bars 99,511-100,508 all show same extreme
  • Creates "boxy" flat-top appearance rather than smooth line
  • Only updates when NEW extreme forms

Fixes needed:

  1. Clamp g_indicator_start_bar:
g_indicator_start_bar = MathMin(g_pivots[i].bar, rates_total - 1);
  1. Use incremental extreme tracking instead of full rescan each bar:
  • Track running extreme in global variable
  • Update only when current bar's high/low exceeds it
  • Reset when new leg starts

I asked Gemini again, here's what it said, but I tried its "fix" but it only made it worse: I have reviewed your code. The primary reason your indicator line isn't "painting" correctly is a mismatch between the Plot Index and the Buffer Index in your `OnInit()` function. In MQL5, `PlotIndexSet...` functions refer to the Plot Number (the visual element), while `SetIndexBuffer` links that plot to a specific data array. Your code defines 10 plots, but you are accidentally trying to set properties for Plot Index 8 and 9 using the wrong logic, and more importantly, your primary data plot is being overwritten by the "None" settings of your debug buffers. 1. The Core Bug: Plot Index Overlap In `OnInit`, you have this: C++ ``` SetIndexBuffer(1, Combined_Buffer, INDICATOR_DATA); SetIndexBuffer(2, Combined_Color_Buffer, INDICATOR_COLOR_INDEX); SetIndexBuffer(3, Debug_PivotIdx, INDICATOR_DATA); // ... The second plot (`indicator_label2`) is a `DRAW_COLOR_LINE`, which requires two buffers (Buffer 1 for data, Buffer 2 for color). Because Plot 2 uses two buffers, Plot 3 starts at Buffer Index 3. Your code was disabling Plot 2 (your main line) because you were calling `PlotIndexSetInteger(2, DRAW_NONE)` thinking it referred to the PivotIdx buffer, but it actually referred to the Reversal Probability plot. Claude2026-01-09 22:13 UTC

Trade arrow placement logic:

  1. Starting point determination:

    • If Trade_Start_Date = 0: Find first confirmed pivot where pivot.bar >= g_indicator_start_bar
    • If Trade_Start_Date ≠ 0: Find first confirmed pivot where time[pivot.bar] >= Trade_Start_Date
    • This becomes start_pivot_idx

  2. Arrow placement:

    • Loop through confirmed pivots from start_pivot_idx to confirmed_count - 2 (excludes tentative last pivot)
    • Each trade: entry at pivot[i], exit at pivot[i+1]
    • Arrow placed at bar: pivot[i].bar + 1

So arrows start at:

  • The bar matching the first eligible pivot (after indicator start bar or after date filter)
  • And only use confirmed pivots (not the last tentative one)
Millard2026-01-09 22:14 UTC
so you're just planting arrows where ZZ pivots are?
Claude2026-01-09 22:14 UTC

Yes, essentially. Each arrow represents a simulated trade:

Entry: At ZigZag pivot (trough = buy, peak = sell)
Arrow placement: One bar after the pivot (pivot.bar + 1)
Exit: At the next ZigZag pivot
P/L calculation: Distance between entry and exit pivots

So every confirmed ZigZag pivot gets an arrow (except the first and last).

Is this the wrong approach? Should arrows be placed based on indicator confidence level instead?

Millard2026-01-09 22:19 UTC
OK, I'm sure you can see what that's worthless from a trading decision perspective. Here's the dependency chain: ZZs have NOTHING to do with trading decisions. They only serve as data for the historical distribution. They inform the indicator. The indicator line is supposed to show how close the price has moved toward the ZZ spread threshold determined by the max value found in the selected percentile of the historical distribution. There is no direct logical link from ZZ to trades. Trades are based on logic that analyzed the indicator line itself. The higher the line (closer to max spread threshold), the closer price is getting to a historically probable pivot. We don't know what that trade logic will be until we can see an accurate indicator line and see if it suggests signals. Also, as you can see, the labels still aren't being correctly vertically spaced. They're blocked by the arrows.

   g_pivot_count = 0;
   g_last_pivot_bar = -1;
   g_last_pivot_price = 0.0;
   g_leg_count = 0;
   g_distribution_ready = false;
   g_indicator_start_bar = 0;
   g_distribution_start_pivot_idx = 0;
   g_inwork_active = false;
   g_inwork_leg_start_bar = -1;
   g_inwork_leg_start_price = 0.0;
   g_inwork_direction = 0;
   g_inwork_extreme = 0.0;
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   g_alerts_armed = false;
   g_last_bar_time = 0;
   g_csv_exported = false;
   g_initial_build_done = false;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
   
   Print("HoldConfidence v8.10 initialized - Min_ZZ_Legs: ", Min_ZZ_Legs);
   
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   if(zz_handle != INVALID_HANDLE)
      IndicatorRelease(zz_handle);
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   ObjectDelete(0, g_status_label_name);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipSize()
{
   return (_Digits == 3 || _Digits == 5) ? _Point * 10 : _Point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void InsertSorted(double &arr[], int &count, double value)
{
   ArrayResize(arr, count + 1);
   int i = count;
   while(i > 0 && arr[i-1] > value)
   {
      arr[i] = arr[i-1];
      i--;
   }
   arr[i] = value;
   count++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPercentileValue(const double &arr[], int count, double percentile)
{
   if(count <= 0) return 0.0;
   if(percentile <= 0.0) return arr[0];
   if(percentile >= 100.0) return arr[count - 1];
   
   double rank = (percentile / 100.0) * (count - 1);
   int lo = (int)MathFloor(rank);
   int hi = (int)MathCeil(rank);
   
   if(lo < 0) lo = 0;
   if(hi >= count) hi = count - 1;
   if(hi == lo) return arr[lo];
   
   double frac = rank - lo;
   return arr[lo] + (arr[hi] - arr[lo]) * frac;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string FormatWithCommas(double value, int decimals = 1)
{
   string str = DoubleToString(MathAbs(value), decimals);
   string sign = (value < 0) ? "-" : "";
   
   int dot_pos = StringFind(str, ".");
   string int_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, 0, dot_pos) : str;
   string dec_part = (dot_pos >= 0) ? StringSubstr(str, dot_pos) : "";
   
   string result = "";
   int len = StringLen(int_part);
   for(int i = 0; i < len; i++)
   {
      if(i > 0 && (len - i) % 3 == 0)
         result += ",";
      result += StringSubstr(int_part, i, 1);
   }
   return sign + result + dec_part;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(int rates_total)
{
   double zz_buffer[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(zz_buffer, false);
   
   int copied = CopyBuffer(zz_handle, 0, 0, rates_total, zz_buffer);
   if(copied <= 0)
   {
      Print("Failed to copy ZigZag buffer: ", GetLastError());
      return;
   }
   
   ArrayResize(g_pivots, 0);
   g_pivot_count = 0;
   
   for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
   {
      if(zz_buffer[i] != 0.0 && zz_buffer[i] != EMPTY_VALUE)
      {
         ArrayResize(g_pivots, g_pivot_count + 1);
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].bar = i;
         g_pivots[g_pivot_count].price = zz_buffer[i];
         g_pivot_count++;
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void BuildDistribution(int rates_total)
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2)
   {
      g_leg_count = 0;
      g_distribution_ready = false;
      return;
   }
   
   ArrayResize(g_leg_sizes, 0);
   g_leg_count = 0;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   
   int target_legs = (Min_ZZ_Legs == 0) ? (confirmed_count - 1) : Min_ZZ_Legs;
   int legs_with_priming = target_legs + PRIMING_BUFFER;
   
   int start_pivot_idx = confirmed_count - 1;
   int legs_counted = 0;
   
   for(int i = start_pivot_idx; i > 0 && legs_counted < legs_with_priming; i--)
   {
      double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_pivots[i].price - g_pivots[i-1].price) / pip_size;
      if(leg_size_pips > 0)
      {
         InsertSorted(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, leg_size_pips);
         legs_counted++;
         
         if(!g_initial_build_done && (legs_counted % 10 == 0 || legs_counted >= target_legs))
         {
            int display_count = MathMin(legs_counted, target_legs);
            double pct = MathMin(100.0, (display_count * 100.0) / target_legs);
            string status = StringFormat("Building distribution... %d/%d legs (%.1f%%)", 
                                        display_count, target_legs, pct);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
         
         if(legs_counted == legs_with_priming)
            g_distribution_start_pivot_idx = i - 1;
      }
   }
   
   if(g_leg_count >= target_legs)
   {
      g_distribution_ready = true;
      
      int legs_for_start = (Min_ZZ_Legs == 0) ? 1 : Min_ZZ_Legs;
      int legs_back = 0;
      for(int i = confirmed_count - 1; i > 0; i--)
      {
         legs_back++;
         if(legs_back >= legs_for_start)
         {
            g_indicator_start_bar = g_pivots[i].bar;
            // CRITICAL FIX: Clamp to valid range
            if(g_indicator_start_bar >= rates_total)
               g_indicator_start_bar = rates_total - 1;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RebuildMockTrades(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(!Enter_Mock_Trades) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 3) return;
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   
   g_cum_pl = 0.0;
   g_cum_gain = 0.0;
   g_cum_loss = 0.0;
   g_last_trade_pivot_idx = -1;
   
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_Arrow_");
   ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "HoldConf_PL_");
   
   int confirmed_count = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int start_pivot_idx = 0;
   
   if(Trade_Start_Date == 0)
   {
      for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count; i++)
      {
         if(g_pivots[i].bar >= g_indicator_start_bar)
         {
            start_pivot_idx = i;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
   else
   {
      for(int i = 0; i < confirmed_count; i++)
      {
         if(time[g_pivots[i].bar] >= Trade_Start_Date)
         {
            start_pivot_idx = i;
            break;
         }
      }
   }
   
   int total_trades = confirmed_count - 1 - start_pivot_idx;
   int trades_placed = 0;
   
   for(int i = start_pivot_idx; i < confirmed_count - 1; i++)
   {
      PlaceMockTrade(i, i + 1, rates_total, time, high, low);
      g_last_trade_pivot_idx = i;
      trades_placed++;
      
      if(!g_initial_build_done && (trades_placed % 50 == 0 || i == confirmed_count - 2))
      {
         string status = StringFormat("Placing trade arrows... %d/%d (%.1f%%)", 
                                     trades_placed, total_trades,
                                     (trades_placed * 100.0) / total_trades);
         UpdateStatusMeter(status);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceMockTrade(int entry_idx, int exit_idx, int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(entry_idx < 0 || exit_idx >= g_pivot_count) return;
   
   int entry_bar = g_pivots[entry_idx].bar;
   int exit_bar = g_pivots[exit_idx].bar;
   double entry_price = g_pivots[entry_idx].price;
   double exit_price = g_pivots[exit_idx].price;
   
   bool is_buy = true;
   if(entry_idx > 0)
   {
      double prev_price = g_pivots[entry_idx - 1].price;
      is_buy = (entry_price < prev_price);
   }
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double pl_pips = is_buy ? (exit_price - entry_price) / pip_size
                           : (entry_price - exit_price) / pip_size;
   
   g_cum_pl += pl_pips;
   if(pl_pips >= 0)
      g_cum_gain += pl_pips;
   else
      g_cum_loss += pl_pips;
   
   int arrow_bar = entry_bar + 1;
   if(arrow_bar >= rates_total) arrow_bar = rates_total - 1;
   
   datetime arrow_time = time[arrow_bar];
   double arrow_offset = TradeMarkerOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   double label_offset = TradeLabelOffsetPips * GetPipSize();
   
   string trade_id = IntegerToString(entry_bar);
   string arrow_name = "HoldConf_Arrow_" + trade_id;
   string label_name = "HoldConf_PL_" + trade_id;
   
   double arrow_price, label_price;
   
   if(is_buy)
   {
      arrow_price = low[arrow_bar] - arrow_offset;
      label_price = arrow_price - label_offset;  // Offset FROM arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_TOP);
   }
   else
   {
      arrow_price = high[arrow_bar] + arrow_offset;
      label_price = arrow_price + label_offset;  // Offset FROM arrow
      
      ObjectCreate(0, arrow_name, OBJ_ARROW, 0, arrow_time, arrow_price);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
      ObjectSetInteger(0, arrow_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
   }
   
   string pl_abs = FormatWithCommas(MathAbs(pl_pips), 0);
   string pl_text = (pl_pips < 0) ? "(" + pl_abs + ")" : pl_abs;
   color pl_color = is_buy ? clrLime : clrRed;
   
   ObjectCreate(0, label_name, OBJ_TEXT, 0, arrow_time, label_price);
   ObjectSetString(0, label_name, OBJPROP_TEXT, pl_text);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_COLOR, pl_color);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_ANCHOR, ANCHOR_RIGHT);
   ObjectSetInteger(0, label_name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
   
   if(Alert_On_Trade_Arrow && g_alerts_armed)
   {
      bool is_realtime = (arrow_bar >= rates_total - 2);
      if(!Alert_Only_Realtime || is_realtime)
      {
         string side = is_buy ? "BUY" : "SELL";
         Alert("HoldConfidence: ", side, " @ ", _Symbol, " ", TimeToString(arrow_time, TIME_DATE|TIME_MINUTES));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateInWorkLeg(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   int last_pivot_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int leg_start_bar = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].bar;
   double leg_start_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx].price;
   
   // Check if new leg started
   if(!g_inwork_active || g_inwork_leg_start_bar != leg_start_bar)
   {
      // Determine direction from previous pivot
      g_inwork_direction = 0;
      if(last_pivot_idx > 0)
      {
         double prev_price = g_pivots[last_pivot_idx - 1].price;
         if(leg_start_price > prev_price)
            g_inwork_direction = -1;  // Peak -> down leg
         else if(leg_start_price < prev_price)
            g_inwork_direction = 1;   // Trough -> up leg
      }
      
      g_inwork_active = true;
      g_inwork_leg_start_bar = leg_start_bar;
      g_inwork_leg_start_price = leg_start_price;
      g_inwork_extreme = leg_start_price;
   }
   
   // Update extreme incrementally
   if(g_inwork_direction > 0)
   {
      if(high[bar] > g_inwork_extreme)
         g_inwork_extreme = high[bar];
   }
   else if(g_inwork_direction < 0)
   {
      if(low[bar] < g_inwork_extreme)
         g_inwork_extreme = low[bar];
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicatorForBar(int bar, const double &high[], const double &low[], int rates_total)
{
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = EMPTY_VALUE;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = 0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = -1;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = 0;
   Debug_LegDistCount[bar] = g_leg_count;
   Debug_PivotCount[bar] = g_pivot_count;
   CumulativePL[bar] = g_cum_pl;
   CumulativeGain[bar] = g_cum_gain;
   CumulativeLoss[bar] = g_cum_loss;
   
   if(!g_distribution_ready) return;
   if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;
   if(g_pivot_count < 2) return;
   
   UpdateInWorkLeg(bar, high, low);
   
   if(!g_inwork_active || g_inwork_direction == 0) return;
   
   double pip_size = GetPipSize();
   double leg_size_pips = MathAbs(g_inwork_extreme - g_inwork_leg_start_price) / pip_size;
   
   double target = GetPercentileValue(g_leg_sizes, g_leg_count, ConfidenceTargetPercentile);
   if(target <= 0) return;
   
   double confidence = MathMin(100.0, 100.0 * (leg_size_pips / target));
   
   Historic_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Buffer[bar] = confidence;
   Combined_Color_Buffer[bar] = (g_inwork_direction > 0) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
   Debug_PivotIdx[bar] = g_inwork_leg_start_bar;
   Debug_LegSize[bar] = leg_size_pips;
   Debug_LegStart[bar] = g_inwork_leg_start_price;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RecalculateFromLegStart(int leg_start_bar, int rates_total, const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   // Reset in-work leg on recalculation
   g_inwork_active = false;
   
   for(int i = leg_start_bar; i < rates_total; i++)
      CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForPivotChanges(int rates_total, const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[])
{
   if(g_pivot_count == 0) return;
   
   int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
   int current_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
   double current_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
   
   if(g_last_pivot_bar < 0)
   {
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
      return;
   }
   
   if(current_bar != g_last_pivot_bar || MathAbs(current_price - g_last_pivot_price) > _Point)
   {
      Print("Last pivot CHANGED: was bar ", g_last_pivot_bar, 
            " @ ", DoubleToString(g_last_pivot_price, _Digits),
            " now bar ", current_bar,
            " @ ", DoubleToString(current_price, _Digits));
      
      BuildDistribution(rates_total);
      RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      int recalc_from = MathMin(g_last_pivot_bar, current_bar);
      RecalculateFromLegStart(recalc_from, rates_total, high, low);
      
      g_last_pivot_bar = current_bar;
      g_last_pivot_price = current_price;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnCalculate(const int rates_total,
                const int prev_calculated,
                const datetime &time[],
                const double &open[],
                const double &high[],
                const double &low[],
                const double &close[],
                const long &tick_volume[],
                const long &volume[],
                const int &spread[])
{
   if(rates_total < 10)
   {
      Print("Insufficient bars: ", rates_total);
      return 0;
   }
   
   bool is_new_bar = false;
   if(rates_total > 0)
   {
      datetime current = time[rates_total - 1];
      if(g_last_bar_time == 0)
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      else if(current != g_last_bar_time)
      {
         is_new_bar = true;
         g_last_bar_time = current;
      }
   }
   
   if(!g_initial_build_done)
   {
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD START ===");
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Extracting ZigZag pivots...");
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      Print("Extracted ", g_pivot_count, " pivots");
      
      BuildDistribution(rates_total);
      Print("Distribution: ", g_leg_count, " legs, ready: ", g_distribution_ready, " start_bar: ", g_indicator_start_bar);
      
      if(Enter_Mock_Trades)
         RebuildMockTrades(rates_total, time, high, low);
      
      UpdateStatusMeter("Calculating indicator values...");
      
      for(int i = 0; i < rates_total; i++)
      {
         CalculateIndicatorForBar(i, high, low, rates_total);
         
         if(i % 1000 == 0 || i == rates_total - 1)
         {
            string status = StringFormat("Calculating... %d/%d bars (%.1f%%)",
                                        i + 1, rates_total,
                                        ((i + 1) * 100.0) / rates_total);
            UpdateStatusMeter(status);
         }
      }
      
      if(g_pivot_count > 0)
      {
         int last_idx = g_pivot_count - 1;
         g_last_pivot_bar = g_pivots[last_idx].bar;
         g_last_pivot_price = g_pivots[last_idx].price;
      }
      
      g_initial_build_done = true;
      g_alerts_armed = true;
      RemoveStatusMeter();
      
      Print("=== INITIAL BUILD COMPLETE ===");
      
      return rates_total;
   }
   
   if(is_new_bar)
   {
      ExtractPivotsFromZigZag(rates_total);
      CheckForPivotChanges(rates_total, time, high, low);
   }
   
   int current = rates_total - 1;
   CalculateIndicatorForBar(current, high, low, rates_total);
   
   return rates_total;
}

Prompt for Opus:

You're taking over an MQL5 indicator project (HoldConfidence v8.10) that has a critical bug preventing the indicator line from drawing. Here's the complete context:

CORE CONCEPT:

  • ZigZag pivots → build historical distribution of leg sizes → calculate indicator showing how close current price is to historical pivot threshold
  • Indicator line should show 0-100% confidence that a reversal is approaching
  • Trade arrows (currently included but not the focus) should eventually be based on indicator signals, NOT ZigZag pivots directly

CURRENT BUG: Indicator line displays as horizontal at 100. Data Window shows all values frozen/identical across all bars. Debug values:

  • PivotIdx: 100512 (out of bounds - rates_total is ~100508)
  • LegSize: 356.2 (never changes)
  • All other values static

ROOT CAUSE IDENTIFIED: g_indicator_start_bar is being set >= rates_total, causing this check to fail for ALL bars:

if(bar < g_indicator_start_bar) return;

Result: CalculateIndicatorForBar() exits immediately for every bar, nothing draws.

ARCHITECTURE (CORRECT):

  1. Extract ALL ZigZag pivots (including last tentative one)
  2. Build distribution from CONFIRMED pivots (all except last) counted BACKWARD from current
  3. Min_ZZ_Legs = threshold (300 default, 0 = all history)
  4. Count back from current until Min_ZZ_Legs accumulated
  5. Add PRIMING_BUFFER (50) extra legs for stable calculations
  6. In-work leg uses LAST pivot (tentative, mutable) with incremental extreme tracking
  7. Indicator outputs confidence = (current_leg_size / distribution_percentile) * 100, capped at 100

KEY VALIDATION NEEDED:

  • Pivot bar indices must be < rates_total during extraction
  • g_indicator_start_bar must be < rates_total after calculation
  • When counting backward Min_ZZ_Legs, track actual pivot bar indices, not just count

WHAT'S BEEN TRIED (FAILED):

  • Clamping g_indicator_start_bar to rates_total - 1 (creates horizontal line at last bar)
  • Various validation approaches in BuildDistribution()
  • v8.11 with more validation (same result)

THE FIX: The backward counting logic in BuildDistribution() is setting g_indicator_start_bar to an invalid value. Need to:

  1. Validate pivot bar indices during backward count
  2. Ensure g_indicator_start_bar lands on a valid bar index
  3. Possibly the loop is accessing pivots with bad bar values

CODE PROVIDED: v8.10 attached - this is the version to fix. DO NOT remove mock trade code.

DELIVERABLE: Fix v8.10 so indicator line draws properly with changing values across bars. Version number → 8.12.
